It is a creature spell (sorcery speed, their main phase only), and Evoke {W/B}{W/B} can be paid {B}{B}, so the three -1/-1 counters mode is a 2-mana play. Two copies of a 2-mana kill for Tidebinder / Wasp / Drowner / Siren / Beavers; plan blanks to last a turn cycle, not forever.
Haliya, Guided by Light (Warp): Counter the warp exile at their end step. Warp's "Exile this creature at the beginning of the next end step" is a delayed trigger created when they cast her for {W}; countering it keeps their 3/3 on the battlefield for one mana (blank only while Tidebinder lives, then a full Haliya). You would be doing them a favour.
Leonardo, Cutting Edge (Sneak): Counter Sneak as he comes in tapped and attacking. Sneak is an alternative cost for casting a spell and "enters tapped and attacking" is a replacement; neither is an ability on the stack. Spell Snare does counter the Leonardo spell (MV 2 regardless of Sneak). Tidebinder can only hit his growth trigger afterward.
Case of the Uneaten Feast / Lunar Convocation: Blank the enchantment engine. Both are enchantments; Tidebinder's ruling checks the source type on resolution and an enchantment never loses abilities. You only counter that one instance (1 life, one end-step drain, one Bat, one draw). Creatures cast from the graveyard after a Solved Case are spells, not abilities.
Amalia Benavides Aguirre (the 20-power wrath): Hold Tidebinder for the "destroy all other creatures if its power is exactly 20" moment. It is part of the explore trigger, so it is counterable, but without Wildgrowth Walker this list essentially never reaches exactly 20 power. The wrath is not what beats you.
Curious Farm Animals: Countering the sac ability also stops the 3 life, or countering the first activation saves Enduring Curiosity. Sacrificing is a cost, so "When this creature dies, you gain 3 life" already triggered and is a separate object on the stack; one Tidebinder counters one of them. And a creature-form Curiosity that is destroyed comes back as an enchantment on its own; only the activation aimed at the ENCHANTMENT form (or a second one) actually removes the draw engine.
Godless Shrine, Shattered Sanctum, Concealed Courtyard, Starting Town, Leyline of the Void: Counter the enters-tapped / pay-2-life / Leyline effects. "As this land enters, you may pay 2 life" and "enters tapped unless" are replacement effects; Leyline's "If a card would be put into an opponent's graveyard... exile it instead" is a replacement effect and its opening-hand clause is a static rule. Starting Town's "{T}, Pay 1 life: Add one mana of any color" is a mana ability. None can be targeted.
Enduring Tenacity (return trigger): Wait to counter the return when it dies. It works (the return trigger is counterable and the card stays in the graveyard), but the source is a card in the graveyard so nothing is blanked; you spent Tidebinder as a one-shot. Blank it first (counter a drain) and kill it the same turn instead.
Ultima (sideboard, 2): Stack several blanks post-board and assume they hold. "Destroy all artifacts and creatures. End the turn" is a sorcery with nothing to counter by ability; it kills Tidebinder, Wasp and every blank at once (Curiosity and their Tenacity come back as enchantments). Post-board, do not over-invest in the board; keep a Kaito or Curiosity in hand.
Not worth it
Manufactured under-Voice window (kill Curious Farm Animals on your turn with 3 extra mana up so its "you gain 3 life" fires Amalia/Cleric triggers you can counter): Hex 1 + Tidebinder 3 on your own turn, gifts 3 life and a drain, blanks one payoff that Erode undoes for {W}, and Farm Animals is a main-deck 1-of. Only if you were killing it anyway and Tidebinder would rot in hand.Pest token "Whenever this token attacks, you gain 1 life": one life on their turn; blanking a 1/1 token is not worth the card.Lunar Convocation: end-step drain, "if you gained and lost life this turn, create a 1/1 black Bat creature token with flying" (a Zoraline Bat and a Wasp/Siren blocker) and "{1}{B}, Pay 2 life: Draw a card" are all counterable but the source is an enchantment (no blank); 1-for-1 at best, only if you were casting Tidebinder as a body anyway.Case of the Uneaten Feast "Whenever a creature you control enters, you gain 1 life": one life, no blank; hit the payoff the life would feed instead.Dalkovan Encampment "{2}{W}, {T}: Whenever you attack this turn, create two 1/1 red Warrior creature tokens": land, no blank, and it is their turn (Voice).Petrified Hamlet "When this land enters, choose a land card name": counterable and leaves the Hamlet nameless (its lock then does nothing, so Restless Reef stays safe), but it is their main phase; take it only when unlocked and nothing better is on the stack.Amalia's Ward trigger against your own WASP: Tidebinder counters it and blanks her, but that is 5 mana of flash for what Tidebinder alone does against any Amalia trigger. (The Drowner / Kaito -2 version is a real target because it is an our-turn window under Voice; see Amalia.)Essence Channeler's die trigger when it carries 1-2 counters: too small to spend the card on.Decorum Dissertation (sideboard 1): nothing to reliably counter; the Paradigm recast is a spell being cast, and it happens in their first main phase under Voice. Skip.Pyrrhic Strike, Strategic Betrayal, Ancient Vendetta, Ultima, Erode, Shoot the Sheriff: spells, no abilities to counter; they are the reasons a blank lasts about one turn cycle. Ancient Vendetta can name Tishana's Tidebinder or The Wondrous Wasp and strip all copies from hand/library/graveyard, but Kaito or Enduring Curiosity are the likelier names.
The Wondrous Wasp
Wasp's job here is the things Tidebinder cannot touch and the turn Tidebinder cannot act on. Cast on OUR main phase it blanks a static lock (Clarion Conqueror when you need Kaito's loyalty, Drowner's shuffle or a Map right now; Voice of Victory when you hold no kill spell for it), or pre-blanks their ONLY gain source (Sanctifier / Haliya) so their whole next turn produces no triggers, all without needing a their-turn window. It is NOT the default answer to Voice: Voice is a 1/3 Human Bard with MV 2, so Requiting Hex ({B}) kills it permanently for 1 mana, and Shoot the Sheriff / Bitter Triumph also do, all castable on our own turn under the lock; Wasp's blank costs 2, is undone by Erode ({W}), {B}{B} Emptiness or Shoot the Sheriff, and needs backup. Secondary jobs: tap-and-blank a payoff before attackers or before a gain chain (Amalia costs 3 life to target; Cleric, Zoraline, Leonardo are free), and blank Enduring Tenacity so it dies for good. No hexproof in the 75; the only ward is Amalia's. Every blank ends the moment the 2/1 leaves.
#1Clarion Conqueror
WhyBlanks "Activated abilities of artifacts, creatures, and planeswalkers can't be activated", re-enabling Kaito's -2 / 0 / +1, Drowner's shuffle, Map explores and sideboard Ghost Vacuum, and removes flying from their best flying blocker. Requiting Hex cannot kill it (MV 3); if you have Shoot the Sheriff / Bitter Triumph, use that instead and keep Wasp for a payoff. Wasp it only on a turn you need an activation immediately and make that activation the same turn.
WhenYour main phase 1, before the Kaito / Drowner / Map activation. Conqueror does not stop Wasp's ETB (triggered).
AfterVanilla tapped 3/3 while Wasp lives. Your activations work only while Wasp is on the battlefield.
#2Voice of Victory (only when you hold no Hex / Sheriff / Triumph)
WhyBlanks the static lock so you can cast spells on their turn again (Tidebinder, Shoot the Sheriff, flash Curiosity) and removes Mobilize. This is the fallback, not the plan: Requiting Hex kills Voice for {B} permanently, Wasp for 2 mana temporarily. The tap is a footnote (Voice has no flying or reach, so it never blocks Beavers / Siren / Wasp, and a ninjutsu Kaito arrives after blockers; it only matters for an already-on-battlefield Kaito or a ground body).
WhenYour main phase 1 on a turn you can also keep one cheap kill spell ({B} Hex or {1}{B} Sheriff) for the response: Wasp 2 + Hex 1 = 3 lands. Do not expect Tidebinder mana in the same cycle at 4-5 lands; the affordable unlock that leaves 3 up for Tidebinder is Hex on Voice.
AfterLoses the lock and Mobilize; keeps 1/3, tapped until their untap. The lock returns instantly if Wasp dies (Erode in their upkeep is the standard answer), but their removal is on the stack BEFORE the lock returns, so respond by killing Voice.
#3Hinterland Sanctifier / Haliya, Guided by Light (when it is their only gain source)
WhyCast on OUR turn under Voice, blanking the only fuel on the battlefield means their next turn's creatures produce no lifegain events: no explores, no drain, no Infusion (Infusion needs "if you gained life this turn"), no Haliya draw. This is the way to shut off payoffs when Tidebinder cannot be cast on their turn. Haliya is also a 3/3 blocker worth tapping.
WhenYour main phase (or your end step for a blocker) when Sanctifier or Haliya is the only gain source and Case of the Uneaten Feast is not on the battlefield (Case is an enchantment Wasp cannot touch and keeps feeding gains).
AfterSanctifier vanilla 1/2; Haliya vanilla 3/3. Life already gained stays.
#4Enduring Tenacity (post-board only, 1 copy)
WhyTap-and-blank, then kill it with Shoot the Sheriff / Bitter Triumph the same turn: with no abilities when it dies, the "return it to the battlefield... It's an enchantment" trigger never happens. Permanent answer to a card built to beat removal.
WhenAny turn you also have removal mana; our turn under Voice is fine. Kill it before Wasp leaves.
AfterVanilla 4/3 until dead; gone for good if killed while blank.
#5Amalia Benavides Aguirre
WhyBlank stops explores and removes ward, so follow-up removal (Requiting Hex, Shoot the Sheriff, Kaito -2, Drowner) is untaxed while Wasp lives. Tapping before attackers stops a grown Amalia swinging.
WhenTheir beginning of combat (pre-attackers) when unlocked, or your main phase for the ward-strip-then-kill line. Wasp's trigger targets Amalia, so Ward triggers: pay 3 life or Wasp's trigger is countered (Wasp still enters as a 2/1 flier). Only worth 3 life if the kill follows immediately.
AfterLoses ward and explore; keeps all +1/+1 counters. Ward returns when Wasp leaves.
#6Starscape Cleric
WhyBlank the drain, their clock against us. No ward.
WhenBefore a gain chain: their main phase pre-cast when unlocked, or your turn to pre-empt. Tapping it means little (it cannot block anyway; blanked it CAN block).
After2/1 with no flying and no drain.
#7Zoraline, Cosmos Caller
WhyTapped before attackers she cannot attack, so no attack-trigger recursion and no "Whenever a Bat you control attacks, you gain 1 life"; the blank strips flying / vigilance so she stops blocking Wasp / Siren / Beavers.
WhenTheir beginning of combat when unlocked; or your end step to pre-empt.
AfterVanilla 3/3 while Wasp lives.
#8Moseo, Vein's New Dean
WhyA blanked Moseo has no Infusion at their end step and no flying. Wasp on your turn carries the blank through their whole next turn if Wasp survives; useful because Shoot the Sheriff / Hex cannot kill him.
WhenYour turn, or their second main phase before the end step (unlocked only).
AfterVanilla 2/1; a Pest already made keeps its ability.
#9Leonardo, Cutting Edge
WhyLoses lifelink (a gain source nothing else switches off) and growth; tap removes an attacker or blocker.
WhenYour main phase before attackers, or their beginning of combat when unlocked.
AfterKeeps counters, no lifelink or growth.
Traps
Voice of Victory: Wasp is the default answer to Voice, or Kaito -2 (tap + two stun counters) lifts the lock. Stun and tap do nothing to a static ability. And Requiting Hex ({B}, Voice is MV 2), Shoot the Sheriff and Bitter Triumph all kill Voice permanently on our own turn; Wasp's 2-mana blank ends to Erode ({W}) or {B}{B} Emptiness. Kill it if you can; Wasp it only when you cannot.
Voice of Victory: Once Wasp blanks Voice the lock is handled, and you can Wasp now then Tidebinder plus a kill spell on their turn. The lock returns the instant Wasp leaves; expect Erode in their upkeep. Mana spent on our main phase does not come back: Wasp (2) + Tidebinder (3) + a backup kill (1-2) is a 6-7 land line. At 4-5 lands, pair Wasp with exactly one cheap kill spell for the response and skip the Tidebinder plan that cycle, or better, Hex the Voice and hold 3 for Tidebinder.
Any attacker: Flash Wasp after attackers are declared to stop the attack. Tapping does not remove a creature from combat. Resolve Wasp in their beginning of combat step (or your end step) to stop an attack; mid-combat it only blanks (Leonardo loses lifelink; Zoraline's attack trigger is already on the stack and still resolves).
Amalia Benavides Aguirre: Wasp is a free tap on Amalia. Wasp targets the creature, so Ward-Pay 3 life triggers; if you decline, Wasp's trigger is countered and you get a plain 2/1 flier. Tidebinder on her explore (or on the ward trigger itself) is the ward-free route.
Clarion Conqueror: You must Wasp the Conqueror before Tidebinder or Wasp can work. Conqueror stops activated abilities only; Tidebinder's and Wasp's effects are triggered abilities and work through it. It also does not stop Kaito's ninjutsu (an ability of a card in hand, not of a permanent) or Restless Reef (a land). Wasp it only when you need Kaito's loyalty abilities, Drowner's shuffle or Map tokens that turn.
Case of the Uneaten Feast / Lunar Convocation: Blank the enchantments. Wasp targets creatures only. With Case on the battlefield, blanking Sanctifier does not stop the gains.
Essence Channeler: Blanking shrinks it. It keeps its +1/+1 counters; it only loses the conditional flying / vigilance, the growth trigger and the death counter-transfer. Still an X/X body.
Lines with the rest of the deck
  • Cheap unlock, then Tidebinder: on your main phase Requiting Hex ({B}) kills Voice of Victory (MV 2, Human Bard) permanently; hold 3 for Tidebinder on their turn. Four lands, and it keeps Kaito / Curiosity mana intact next turn. Shoot the Sheriff / Bitter Triumph do the same for 2.
  • End-step decision at 4 mana: from turn 4 on, pass with 4 up on their turn. At their end step, if Moseo's Infusion (target already chosen) or Haliya's "draw a card if you've gained 3 or more life" is on the stack, Tidebinder it; if nothing worth countering appears, flash in Enduring Curiosity. Needs Voice absent or dead.
  • Ward strip under Voice (our turn): Floodpits Drowner ETB or Kaito -2 targets Amalia; her Ward trigger goes on the stack; Tidebinder counters the WARD trigger. Amalia is the ward's source, so she is blanked, you pay no life, and Drowner's "{1}{U}, {T}: Shuffle this creature and target creature with a stun counter" is untaxed too (needs Conqueror absent or blanked for the shuffle). Same line with Tidebinder on her explore when unlocked, then Hex / Sheriff / Kaito -2 her untaxed the same turn.
  • Wasp on Voice only as fallback: if you have no kill spell, Wasp the Voice (tapped, blanked) with exactly one cheap kill spell held ({B} Hex or {1}{B} Sheriff). When they Erode or Sheriff the Wasp in their upkeep, the lock is still off while their spell is on the stack: respond by killing Voice. If instead they evoke Emptiness for {B}{B} at Wasp and you have 3 up, Tidebinder the -1/-1 ETB (the evoke sacrifice still happens) and the lock stays off.
  • Pre-blank the fuel: with Voice out and Hinterland Sanctifier or Haliya as their only gain source (no Case of the Uneaten Feast), Wasp it on your main phase; their next turn's creatures then gain nothing, so Amalia, Cleric, Moseo Infusion and Haliya's draw all stay silent without any their-turn window.
  • Reanimation tax: let Zoraline's enter / attack trigger resolve, let them pay {W}{B} and 2 life, then Tidebinder the reflexive "When you do, return target nonland permanent card" trigger; payment stays spent, nothing returns, Zoraline blanked. Same idea for Moseo's Infusion at their end step (target already chosen, counter before it resolves).
  • Permanent Tenacity kill (post-board): Wasp or Tidebinder blanks Enduring Tenacity, then Shoot the Sheriff / Bitter Triumph it the same turn; a blanked Glimmer has no death trigger and does not come back. Day of Black Sun with X at least 4 does the same by itself ("loses all abilities until end of turn. Destroy those creatures") but ALSO permanently kills your own Enduring Curiosity for the same reason; X=2 or 3 wipes Amalia (no ward, no target), Cleric, Voice, Leonardo, Channeler, Sanctifier, Farm Animals (no 3 life) and tokens, at the cost of your own Siren / Beavers / Wasp / Drowner (X=2) and Tidebinder (X=3).
  • Kaito under their locks: ninjutsu still works (activated from hand, on your turn, not a spell, so neither Voice nor Conqueror stops it); Conqueror stops only his loyalty abilities, so Sheriff / Triumph the Conqueror or Wasp it first if you need -2 that turn. Kaito -2 on Amalia costs 3 life (ward) unless she is blanked; Kaito -2 on Voice does NOT lift the lock.
  • Counter Voice on the stack: Spell Snare hits Voice, Amalia, Starscape Cleric (offspring does not change mana value), Leonardo (even via Sneak), Essence Channeler and Lunar Convocation. Voice's lock is not active while it is a spell. But do not skip a turn-1 Spyglass Siren to hold Snare on the draw; a turn-2 Voice is answered by Requiting Hex on your own turn 2.
  • Vren, the Relentless (sideboard) shuts off the creature-death half of their recursion: their creatures are exiled instead of dying, so Curious Farm Animals' 3 life, Essence Channeler's counter transfer, Moseo Infusion and Case rebuys of creature cards, and Enduring Tenacity's return have nothing to work with. Zoraline can still rebuy a nonland permanent card that reached the graveyard another way (a destroyed Lunar Convocation, a countered spell).
Hold or fire
The whole matchup is decided by whether Voice of Victory is on the battlefield un-blanked and un-killed. (1) Voice not down: FIRE Tidebinder on the first card-saving or mana-sink trigger (Moseo Infusion with its target chosen, Starscape Cleric's offspring ETB or first drain of a chain, Zoraline's paid reflexive trigger, Emptiness's {W}{W} reanimate, Haliya's end-step draw). Take Amalia's explore only when a chain is about to fire with her as the main payoff or you will kill her untaxed the same turn. Do not hold for a better window; every turn Voice may land and close their turn to you. (2) Voice down and un-blanked: Tidebinder is a sorcery-speed 3/2. On your turn, kill Voice first (Requiting Hex, Shoot the Sheriff, Bitter Triumph); Wasp it only if you hold none of those. Cast Tidebinder in your main phase only as a needed blocker or to counter an our-turn trigger (Amalia's Ward trigger when Drowner / Kaito -2 targets her, Channeler's die trigger, Amalia / Cleric triggers off a Leonardo lifelink block). (3) Turn 3 is Kaito's turn: with Kaito and Tidebinder both in hand, ninjutsu Kaito rather than holding 3 on their turn, unless a guaranteed card-saving trigger is coming (Moseo on the battlefield with a creature in their graveyard, or Cleric on the battlefield with two gain sources). From turn 4, holding 4 on their turn covers both Tidebinder and flash Curiosity, so the tension disappears: decide at their end step. (4) Wasp: with Clarion Conqueror out, Wasp it on the turn you need an activation and use it that turn (or Sheriff / Triumph it and keep Wasp). With Voice out and no kill spell, Wasp the Voice with one cheap kill spell held for the response. With Voice out and only Sanctifier / Haliya as gain source, Wasp the fuel on your turn. Without lock pieces, hold Wasp for their beginning of combat (tap Zoraline / Amalia / Leonardo before attackers, blank Cleric before the chain) or to remove a blocker for a Kaito attack; fire it as a plain 2/1 flier only when you need the clock. On the play: your turn-3 Tidebinder is live on their turn 3 unless they landed a turn-2 Voice; if they did, turn 3 is Kaito or Hex-on-Voice and Tidebinder waits. On the draw: cast Siren turn 1, answer a turn-2 Voice with Requiting Hex on your turn 2, and treat Tidebinder as a turn-4+ card once the lock is handled. Never spend Tidebinder on an enchantment or land ability while a creature payoff is on the battlefield with abilities left to trigger. Treat every blank as lasting about one turn cycle: if the blanked creature is worth killing, kill it now.
How many to run
Game 1 stock (1 Tidebinder main, 2 Wasp main) is right. Post-board: 2 Tidebinder if your 75 has them (the MTGGoldfish stock 15 in the data file carries none; the Karsten Sept 3 aggregate carries 2 in the sideboard, so this depends on your own list), keep 2 Wasp, never 3 Tidebinder. Reasoning: Tidebinder's targets are excellent (every payoff is a creature, ward-free Amalia blank, Moseo cannot be Sheriff'd), but three Voice of Victory plus Erode, {B}{B} Emptiness and post-board Shoot the Sheriff / Pyrrhic Strike / Ultima make the counter half dead or undone too often for a third copy. Wasp stays at 2 because it is the only card besides removal that answers Conqueror / Voice on our turn and it pre-blanks fuel under the lock. Suggested swaps (opinion, not oracle): in 1-2 Day of Black Sun, 1 Vren, 1 Strategic Betrayal, plus the second Tidebinder if available; out 2 We Say Thee Nay! (Voice makes reactive spells worse and their key spells cost 1-2) and 2 Wan Shi Tong (flash body hosed by Voice; its search trigger never fires here since Erode makes US search); if a fifth cut is needed, a Spell Snare on the play rather than a Bitter Triumph or a land. Keep both Bitter Triumph: it is the only main-deck spell that kills Moseo (Warlock, Sheriff-proof) and one of few that kill Haliya / Conqueror / Zoraline (MV 3, Hex-proof). Day of Black Sun is symmetric: cast it on an empty or Kaito-only board, X=2 or 3 is the usual number, and X at least 4 permanently kills your own Enduring Curiosity (it loses its return trigger before it is destroyed). Ghost Vacuum is worse than usual here (Conqueror shuts it off, Farm Animals kills it). Flashfreeze stays out (no red or green spells).
Review trail: confidence medium, 6 disputed, 16 changes
Disputed reviewer points (kept as written)
  • Brief statement 'Ward taxes Wasp's trigger and Tidebinder's trigger': rejected for Tidebinder. Ward (CR 702.21b) triggers 'Whenever this permanent becomes the target of a spell or ability an opponent controls'. Tidebinder's trigger targets an activated or triggered ability on the stack, never the permanent, so Amalia's Ward-Pay 3 life does not trigger against it. It does trigger against Wasp, Floodpits Drowner (ETB and shuffle), Kaito -2, Requiting Hex and Shoot the Sheriff, each of which targets Amalia.
  • Reviewer 1 finding 'Wasp is the WORST of our three answers to Voice': accepted in substance (Requiting Hex is {B} and Voice is MV 2 per the deck file), but not for Clarion Conqueror in the same breath: Conqueror is MV 3 ({2}{W}), so Requiting Hex ('Destroy target creature with mana value 2 or less') cannot kill it; only Shoot the Sheriff / Bitter Triumph (Dragon, non-outlaw) or Wasp answer it. Conqueror therefore stays Wasp's top target.
  • Reviewer 1 correction 'fifth slot, if needed, is Spell Snare on the play': accepted as a permissible cut, not as a rule; Spell Snare still hits Amalia, Cleric, Leonardo, Channeler and Convocation (all MV 2 per the deck file) at any point in the game, so it is listed as the last cut rather than the first.
  • Reviewer 2 finding on Decorum Dissertation: accepted; reworded to 'nothing to reliably counter, the copy is a spell'. The oracle text in the data ('you may cast a copy of it from exile... at the beginning of each of your first main phases') does not settle whether the permission is a triggered ability, so no counterability claim is made either way.
  • Hedged, not disputed: Warp's exile being a delayed triggered ability (the brief lists delayed 'at the beginning of the next end step' effects as triggered; the advice, never counter it, holds whether or not it is targetable). Clarion Conqueror not stopping ninjutsu rests on CR 109.2 (an ability that says 'planeswalker' without 'card' means a permanent on the battlefield); MKM release notes say the same for cycling. Voice-on-battlefield frequencies (~40% by their turn 3, ~50% by turn 6) are hypergeometric estimates from 3 copies in 60, not measured data.
  • Mechanical hint block gaps (not errors in the brief, but do not trust the tags): Voice of Victory's Mobilize 2 and its token sacrifice are triggered; Starscape Cleric's offspring token creation is a triggered ETB; Emptiness's evoke sacrifice is triggered; Haliya's warp exile is a delayed trigger; Case of the Uneaten Feast's solve condition is a trigger at their end step; Amalia's ward is a triggered ability. All verified against the oracle text and the keyword rules.
Changes after review
  • Re-ranked Tidebinder targets by 'does the counter cost them a card or a mana sink': Moseo Infusion #1, Cleric offspring/drain #2, Amalia #3 (with the pair-with-a-kill framing), Zoraline #4, Emptiness #5, Haliya end-step draw #6, Tenacity #7 (marked post-board 1-of), Case #8, Farm Animals #9, and collapsed Sanctifier / Channeler / Leonardo / Seam Rip / Haliya ETB into one 'body plays' entry.
  • Downgraded Tidebinder verdict from good to situational and confidence from high to medium, with the Voice-frequency reasoning stated in the summary.
  • Corrected Emptiness: sorcery-speed creature (not instant-speed removal); Evoke {W/B}{W/B} paid {B}{B} makes the three -1/-1 counters a 2-mana play; a 6-mana hardcast paying both WW and BB puts both ETB triggers on the stack and Tidebinder counters only one. Added the '{B}{B} Emptiness at Wasp while Wasp blanks Voice' Tidebinder line.
  • Rewrote the answer-to-Voice hierarchy: Requiting Hex / Shoot the Sheriff / Bitter Triumph on our turn first, Spell Snare on the stack, Wasp only as fallback with exactly one cheap kill spell held; removed the claim that Wasp is the default play under the lock.
  • Fixed the mana math on the unlock line (Wasp + Tidebinder + backup kill is 6-7 lands); replaced the headline combined line with 'Hex the Voice, hold 3 for Tidebinder' (4 lands).
  • Removed 'Voice tap clears a blocker for Beavers / Siren / ninjutsu Kaito' (Voice has no flying or reach; ninjutsu Kaito arrives after blockers); tap is now a footnote.
  • Moved the 'manufacture an under-Voice window via Farm Animals' line from combined_lines to dont_bother with the cost stated.
  • Corrected the Farm Animals / Enduring Curiosity line: countering the first activation into a creature-form Curiosity saves only the body (it returns as an enchantment anyway); counter only when Curiosity is already an enchantment.
  • Added the Kaito turn-3 tension and the turn-4+ end-step decision (Tidebinder vs flash Curiosity) to hold_or_fire.
  • Replaced 'keep U open on turn 1 on the draw' with 'cast Siren turn 1, Hex a turn-2 Voice on your turn 2'.
  • Added missed targets: Haliya's end-step draw as her real window; Amalia's Ward trigger as a Tidebinder target on OUR turn (Drowner ETB / Kaito -2) that works under Voice; Wasp pre-blanking Sanctifier / Haliya as sole gain source on our turn under Voice.
  • Counts: flagged that the stock sideboard in the data file has no Tidebinder (Karsten aggregate does); kept both Bitter Triumph (only main-deck kill for Moseo, MV-3 creatures); added Day of Black Sun symmetry warning including that X at least 4 permanently kills our own Enduring Curiosity; Spell Snare listed as the fifth cut instead of Triumph or a land.
  • Added post-board answer inventory (Pyrrhic Strike, Strategic Betrayal, Ultima, Ancient Vendetta) to how_they_win / traps / dont_bother; added Ultima trap about over-committing blanks; added Mobilize token sacrifice delayed-trigger trap; added Lunar Convocation Bat trigger to dont_bother.
  • Narrowed the Vren line to the creature-death half of their recursion (Zoraline can still rebuy a non-creature permanent card).
  • Reworded Decorum Dissertation entry to make no counterability claim about the Paradigm recast.
  • Noted Restless Reef (a land) still animates under Clarion Conqueror.
vs. Selesnya Landfall3.5% TB: goodWasp: premium▼
Every threat is a landfall trigger: Sazh's Chocobo (+1 counter per land), Mossborn Hydra (double its counters per land, with trample), Mightform Harmonizer (double target creature's power per land), plus Icetill Explorer (extra land drop, replay lands from graveyard, mill) and Earthbender Ascension (ramp + quest counters). Fetch lands (Fabled Passage, Escape Tunnel) give two landfall batches per drop, Erode on their own Elves/Chocobo fetches a basic for another batch, and Explorer lets them replay sacrificed fetches every turn. The kill turn is T3-T4: warp Harmonizer for {2}{G}, play a land, crack a fetch, and a Hydra goes 1 -> 2 -> 4 counters while Harmonizer doubles its power on top (two Harmonizers quadruple). Dyadrine is the late-game grind piece (card + Robot per attack) and Esper Origins flashed back becomes Summon: Esper Maduin, a 4/4 Saga creature whose chapter III is a team +2/+2 trample alpha.
Tishana's Tidebinder
Nearly every meaningful thing this deck does is a triggered ability, so Tidebinder always has a target, and the loss-of-abilities rider is where the real value is: countering one landfall trigger permanently (while Tidebinder lives) turns Harmonizer into a vanilla 4/4, Hydra into a no-trample X/X that stops growing, or Explorer into a 2/4 with no extra land drop and no graveyard lands. It is not premium because each land produces 3-5 separate triggers and you stop exactly one, and because Erode (4 main) and Sheltered by Ghosts (4 side) kill or exile the 3/2 and instantly restore everything. Treat the blank as a window to kill the threat, not as the answer, and hold Spell Pierce / We Say Thee Nay! for the Erode.
#1Mightform Harmonizer
Landfall, Whenever a land you control enters, double the power of target creature you control until end of turn.
WhyThis is the lethal multiplier. On a fetch turn it fires twice (fetch enters, basic enters), turning a 4-counter Hydra into 16 trample power; it can also target Harmonizer itself or a Chocobo. Two Harmonizers double twice per land. Countering one trigger blanks the Harmonizer so every later land this turn (fetch crack, Explorer's extra drop) does nothing.
WhenTheir main phase: land enters, the landfall batch goes on the stack, Harmonizer's trigger has a target. Flash Tidebinder targeting that trigger. If an uncracked Fabled Passage/Escape Tunnel is on the battlefield, expect them to crack it in response to Tidebinder's trigger: the new batch (including a fresh Harmonizer double) goes on top and resolves first. Best moment is therefore the batch from the BASIC after the fetch is already cracked, or a turn where the land they played has no fetch backup. On the play you have 3 lands for their T3; on the draw you do not (use Wasp, see below).
AfterLoses Landfall and Warp; becomes a vanilla 4/4. Keeps its 4/4 body. Hydra's own doubling in the same batch still resolves. If it was warped this turn it is still exiled at end step: the warp exile is a delayed trigger created when the spell was cast, not one of the permanent's own abilities, so blanking does not stop it (and you should not try to, see traps).
RiskErode on Tidebinder (or Sheltered by Ghosts exiling it) restores Harmonizer immediately. Kill Harmonizer with Bitter Triumph/Shoot the Sheriff while it is blank if you can, and hold Spell Pierce for the Erode.
#2Mossborn Hydra
Landfall, Whenever a land you control enters, double the number of +1/+1 counters on this creature.
WhyExponential and permanent: 1 -> 2 -> 4 -> 8 counters across two fetch drops, with trample. Countering a doubling at 2+ counters saves 2+ power for good, and the blank strips trample and all future growth. If you can survive the turn, this is the better long-game target than Harmonizer; if the Harmonizer double is lethal this turn, hit Harmonizer instead.
WhenLandfall batch on the stack. They will order Hydra's counter-doubling to resolve before Harmonizer's power-doubling; you can respond to the whole batch at once. Same fetch-in-response caveat as above.
AfterStays at its current counters (counters are not abilities, and its 0/0 base plus counters keeps it alive), loses Trample and Landfall. 'This creature enters with a +1/+1 counter' is a replacement effect and already happened. Result: a vanilla X/X that Dream Beavers or Spyglass Siren can chump forever and Drowner/Kaito -2 can lock down.
RiskErode/Sheltered on Tidebinder re-enables it and it resumes doubling.
#3Icetill Explorer
Landfall, Whenever a land you control enters, mill a card.
WhyThe mill trigger is the hook: countering it blanks Explorer and strips the two static abilities 'You may play an additional land on each of your turns.' and 'You may play lands from your graveyard.' Those statics are the engine that turns one fetch into two landfall batches every single turn (replay the sacrificed Fabled Passage from the graveyard, crack it again). The mill itself also feeds them (milled lands are playable from the graveyard, milled Esper Origins can be flashed back), so stopping it early is worth a little on its own. Statics can't be countered, so the mill trigger is the only Tidebinder hook.
WhenOnly when a land enters while Explorer is out (mill trigger on the stack); there is no end-step window. Best when it is their first land of the turn (their second drop becomes illegal the moment the blank applies) and before they replay a fetch from the graveyard.
AfterBecomes a vanilla 2/4. Lands already played this turn stay played; a fetch already replayed from the graveyard stays on the battlefield.
RiskLow; it is a 2/4 they rarely protect. Wasp does this job for 2 mana with no trigger needed, so prefer Wasp on Explorer and save Tidebinder for Harmonizer/Hydra.
#4Sazh's Chocobo
Landfall, Whenever a land you control enters, put a +1/+1 counter on this creature.
WhyThe single counter is nothing; the blank is the point. Chocobo is a creature, so countering its trigger freezes it at its current size for as long as Tidebinder lives and removes their cheapest Harmonizer doubling target. Take it only when Chocobo is their only real body and nothing better (Harmonizer/Hydra trigger) is on the stack; usually Wasp's job.
WhenLandfall batch on the stack, their main phase. Same fetch-in-response caveat.
AfterFrozen X/1 (0/1 base plus counters), no more growth.
RiskSpends your one main-deck Tidebinder on a 1-drop; on the play with Harmonizer still in their hand, prefer to hold.
#5Sheltered by Ghosts (sideboard, 4)
When this Aura enters, exile target nonland permanent an opponent controls until this Aura leaves the battlefield.
WhyTheir post-board answer to Kaito (a planeswalker without hexproof on THEIR turn), Enduring Curiosity, or your Tidebinder/Wasp (exiling the blanker restores the blanked threat). Countering the ETB saves the permanent outright.
WhenThe Aura resolves and attaches; its ETB trigger goes on the stack targeting your permanent. Flash Tidebinder then. Tidebinder targets the trigger, not the enchanted creature, so ward {2} does not tax it.
AfterNothing is blanked (source is an Aura). The Aura stays attached: 'Enchanted creature gets +1/+0 and has lifelink and ward {2}', so Wasp/Drowner/Kaito -2 on that creature now costs 2 more (or see the next entry).
RiskNone beyond spending the card; this is the highest-value use post-board when Kaito is the target.
#6Creature wearing Sheltered by Ghosts (ward {2} trigger)
Ward {2}, Whenever this creature becomes the target of a spell or ability an opponent controls, counter it unless that player pays {2}.
WhyA two-for-one when the Aura sits on Harmonizer or Hydra: your Kaito -2 / Drowner ETB / Bitter Triumph targets the creature, its ward trigger goes on the stack, Tidebinder counters the ward trigger. Your original spell or ability resolves untaxed AND the creature is blanked; the lifelink and ward the Aura granted were granted BEFORE Tidebinder's effect began, so they are removed too (only the +1/+0 stays, that is a P/T effect, not an ability). If the creature is a Chocobo or Elves, just pay the {2} instead.
WhenWard trigger on the stack, above your targeting spell/ability.
AfterEnchanted creature is vanilla (keeps +1/+0 and counters) while Tidebinder lives; your removal/tap resolves.
Risk3 mana on top of the spell that triggered it; only for Harmonizer/Hydra.
#7Fabled Passage / Escape Tunnel (either activation)
{T}, Sacrifice this land: Search your library for a basic land card, put it onto the battlefield tapped, then shuffle. (Fabled Passage adds: Then if you control four or more lands, untap that land.) Escape Tunnel also has: {T}, Sacrifice this land: Target creature with power 2 or less can't be blocked this turn.
WhyThe land is sacrificed as a cost and stays sacrificed; countering the search means no basic, no second landfall batch, and a lost land drop: a 3-mana Stone Rain stapled to a 3/2 against a deck that lives on land drops. Countering the Tunnel's unblockable activation (aimed at a 2-power Explorer, Robot or small Hydra that a later Harmonizer landfall will double) costs them the land AND the unblockable.
WhenActivation on the stack. Castable on their T3 only if you are on the play (3 lands), otherwise their T4; by then Harmonizer/Hydra triggers usually outrank it. Only when you are holding 3 mana on their turn with nothing better to do.
AfterNothing is blanked (source is a land, and it is in the graveyard). With Explorer out they can replay the fetch from the graveyard later, so the denial is one land drop, not permanent.
RiskSpending Tidebinder here can leave you without it for the Harmonizer turn.
#8Earthbender Ascension (ETB)
When this enchantment enters, earthbend 2. Then search your library for a basic land card, put it onto the battlefield tapped, then shuffle.
WhyCountering denies a 0/0 haste land creature with two +1/+1 counters, a ramp land, AND the landfall batch that land would produce. Real tempo on their T3 when the board is only Chocobo/Elves.
WhenEnchantment resolves, ETB on the stack, their main phase T2-T3 (you need 3 lands: their T3 on the play).
AfterNothing is blanked (enchantment). It keeps 'Landfall, put a quest counter on this enchantment. When you do, if it has four or more quest counters on it, put a +1/+1 counter on target creature you control. It gains trample until end of turn.' and keeps counting.
RiskUses Tidebinder before the real threats arrive; only take it when you are on the play with nothing else to hold for.
#9Dyadrine, Synthesis Amalgam
Whenever you attack, you may remove a +1/+1 counter from each of two creatures you control. If you do, draw a card and create a 2/2 colorless Robot artifact creature token.
WhyTheir grind engine (card + body every attack). Countered: no card, no Robot, and no counters are removed (the removal is part of the countered resolution). Tidebinder enters during declare attackers, before blockers, so the 3/2 can also block.
WhenDeclare attackers step, trigger on the stack.
AfterLoses Trample and the attack trigger; keeps its +1/+1 counters on a 0/1 base. 'Dyadrine enters with a number of +1/+1 counters on it equal to the amount of mana spent to cast it' is a replacement effect and can't be countered.
RiskIt is a 2-of; do not hold Tidebinder specifically for it.
#10Summon: Esper Maduin (Esper Origins flashed back; 4/4 Enchantment Creature, Saga Elemental)
I, Reveal the top card of your library. If it's a permanent card, put it into your hand. / II, Add {G}{G}. / III, Other creatures you control get +2/+2 and gain trample until end of turn.
WhyOnly exists after they flashback Esper Origins ({3}{G}) from the graveyard: it enters transformed with a finality counter. Chapter abilities are triggered abilities; chapter II is NOT a mana ability (Scryfall ruling: 'Summon: Esper Maduin's second chapter ability isn't a mana ability. It uses the stack and can be responded to.'). Because the permanent is a creature, countering ANY chapter blanks it for as long as Tidebinder lives: counter I as it enters and it never reaches II or III.
WhenChapter I: as it enters (lore counter added on entry). Chapters II/III: as their precombat main phase begins, before attacks; counter III there to stop the team +2/+2 trample alpha.
AfterA 4/4 with no chapters and a finality counter. Lore counters keep being added (that is a Saga rule, not an ability), but under CR 704.5s/714.4 a Saga is only sacrificed for its lore counters if it has one or more chapter abilities, so the blanked 4/4 stays on the battlefield. The moment Tidebinder leaves, its chapters return and it is sacrificed if it has 3+ lore counters; the finality counter is not an ability (Scryfall ruling) so it is exiled instead of going to the graveyard.
RiskRare line; only in long games where they surveil/mill Esper Origins into the graveyard.
#11Surrak, Elusive Hunter (sideboard, 2)
Whenever a creature you control or a creature spell you control becomes the target of a spell or ability an opponent controls, draw a card.
WhyEvery Wasp, Drowner ETB, Kaito -2, Bitter Triumph or Shoot the Sheriff you aim at their creatures draws them a card while Surrak lives. Counter the FIRST draw trigger and Surrak is blank: no more draws, no trample. Tidebinder targeting a trigger does not target a creature, so it does not itself feed Surrak.
WhenThe moment one of your targeted spells/abilities makes a creature of theirs a target, Surrak's trigger goes on the stack above it; flash Tidebinder then, and your original spell still resolves.
AfterBecomes a vanilla 4/3. 'This spell can't be countered' is a static about the spell and never mattered to Tidebinder.
Risk5 mana total with the spell that triggered it; usually only worth it when Surrak would otherwise draw 2+ over the game.
#12Earthbended land (delayed return trigger)
When it dies or is exiled, return it to the battlefield tapped. (Delayed triggered ability created by earthbend, from Ba Sing Se or Earthbender Ascension.)
WhyWhen you kill an earthbended land (Bitter Triumph, Shoot the Sheriff, Day of Black Sun, combat), countering the return keeps it in the graveyard: a Stone Rain rider on your removal. Low value because Icetill Explorer can replay it from the graveyard as a land drop.
WhenThe land dies or is exiled, the delayed trigger goes on the stack; flash Tidebinder then.
AfterNothing is blanked: the delayed trigger's source is the source of the earthbend ability that created it (Ba Sing Se, a land, or Earthbender Ascension, an enchantment), per CR 603.7 (the source of a delayed triggered ability is the source of the spell or ability that created it). The land just stays in the graveyard.
RiskAlmost never worth the card; listed so you know it is legal, not so you do it.
#13Ba Sing Se (earthbend activation)
{2}{G}, {T}: Earthbend 2. Activate only as a sorcery.
WhyCountered: 3 mana plus the land's tap are wasted and no 0/0 haste land with two counters appears. Pure tempo, only when no creature trigger is available.
WhenTheir main phase, activation on the stack.
AfterNothing blanked (land). Its mana ability '{T}: Add {G}' was never targetable anyway.
RiskLow value; skip unless the mana is otherwise wasted.
Traps
Icetill Explorer: Counter 'You may play an additional land' or 'You may play lands from your graveyard'. Both are static abilities and cannot be targeted. The only way to remove them is to counter its mill trigger (which blanks the creature) or Wasp it.
Mightform Harmonizer (Warp): Counter the warp cast, or counter the end-step exile to 'punish' the warp. Casting for a warp cost is casting a spell, not an ability. The end-step exile IS a delayed triggered ability and is technically targetable, but countering it leaves their Harmonizer on the battlefield permanently. Whether Tidebinder's blank would even apply to it is unclear; it does not matter, never do it.
Mossborn Hydra / Dyadrine: Counter 'enters with a +1/+1 counter' / 'enters with counters equal to mana spent' so it comes in as a 0/0 and dies. 'Enters with' is a replacement effect, not a trigger. Nothing to target. And blanking either later does not kill it: counters stay.
Landfall in general: Counter 'the landfall' when a land enters and shut the whole turn off. The land entering is not an ability. Each of Chocobo, Hydra, Harmonizer, Explorer, Ascension (and Nursery post-board) has its own separate trigger. Tidebinder counters exactly one; pick the creature whose trigger, and whose blank, matters most.
Erode / Shared Roots: Counter the basic-land search when they Erode their own Llanowar Elves to ramp. Those are spell effects; Tidebinder cannot touch them (Spell Pierce / We Say Thee Nay! counter the spell for {2} unless they pay). You can only counter the landfall triggers the fetched basic produces.
Llanowar Elves / Hushwood Verge / Ba Sing Se / Temple Garden: Counter '{T}: Add {G}'. Mana abilities can't be targeted (printed on Tidebinder). Wasp can blank Elves, Tidebinder can't touch it.
Esper Origins (front face): Counter 'Surveil 2. You gain 2 life' or the flashback. It is a sorcery being cast; flashback is a casting permission, not an ability. Only the transformed Summon: Esper Maduin's chapter triggers are targets.
Torpor Orb (sideboard, 2): Counter Torpor Orb's effect, or flash Tidebinder in response to the Orb spell to 'get it in under the Orb'. 'Creatures entering don't cause abilities to trigger' is static, and once resolved it switches off Tidebinder's and Wasp's own ETB triggers entirely; under an Orb they are a 3/2 and a 2/1 flyer. Tidebinder flashed in response to the Orb SPELL has nothing to counter (a spell is not an ability), so it gains nothing by racing the Orb; only Wasp does (it can still tap/blank a creature in that window). Answer the Orb with Annul, Spell Snare (MV 2) or Duress.
Temple Garden / Ba Sing Se (entering): Counter 'you may pay 2 life' or 'enters tapped unless you control a basic land'. Replacement effects / static, not triggers.
Sapling Nursery (Affinity for Forests) / Surrak ('This spell can't be countered'): Something Tidebinder interacts with. Cost reduction and uncounterability are statics about the spell. Tidebinder never counters spells, so Surrak's clause is irrelevant; his draw trigger is the target.
Sapling Nursery vs Day of Black Sun: Hold Tidebinder to counter Nursery's '{1}{G}, Exile this enchantment: Treefolk and Forests you control gain indestructible' when they respond to your sweeper. Day of Black Sun reads 'Each creature with mana value X or less loses all abilities until end of turn. Destroy those creatures.' The sweeper strips the granted indestructible itself (its loss-of-abilities effect has the later timestamp) before destroying, and the Treefolk tokens are mana value 0. Their response never saves anything; do not spend 3 mana answering it.
Earthbender Ascension / Sheltered by Ghosts / Rest in Peace / fetch lands / Ba Sing Se: Countering their trigger or activation blanks the permanent. Only artifacts, creatures and planeswalkers lose abilities. Enchantments and lands keep everything; you just stop the one ability. Rest in Peace's exile-instead is a replacement effect and cannot be stopped at all.
Earthbended land (0/0 haste land creature): Blanking it turns it back into a plain land, or blanking it stops it from returning when it dies. 'Becomes a 0/0 creature with haste that's still a land' is an effect on it, not an ability, so a blanked earthbended land is still a creature; it loses haste and its mana ability only. The return ('When it dies or is exiled, return it to the battlefield tapped') is a delayed triggered ability created by earthbend (Scryfall ruling on Ba Sing Se: 'the delayed triggered ability created by earthbend'), not an ability of the land, so the blank cannot remove it and the land still comes back. To keep it dead you must counter that delayed trigger itself when it fires (target 12).
Sheltered by Ghosts (static): Counter '+1/+0, lifelink and ward {2}'. Static grant. Only the exile ETB is a trigger. The ward trigger it grants IS a trigger (target 6).
Day of Black Sun (our sideboard): Sweep with X at 2 or 3 while a Tidebinder/Wasp blank is doing work. 'Each creature with mana value X or less loses all abilities until end of turn. Destroy those creatures.' hits our own Wasp (MV 2) and Tidebinder (MV 3) too: they die, and every blank on their side ends with them. X=1 kills Chocobo/Elves/earthbended lands (MV 0-1) and keeps our blankers; X=2 kills Hydra (MV 2) but takes Wasp with it.
Not worth it
Earthbender Ascension's quest counter / reflexive '+1/+1 counter and trample' trigger: one counter and one turn of trample; only if that trample is the difference for lethal.Sapling Nursery's landfall token trigger: one 3/4 reach denied while Nursery keeps making more; and its indestructible activation is irrelevant against Day of Black Sun (see traps). Do not board Tidebinder in for Nursery.Rest in Peace ETB 'exile all graveyards': our list barely uses the graveyard and the ongoing replacement effect can't be stopped.Escape Tunnel's unblockable activation on its own: only matters when a Harmonizer landfall is about to double the unblocked 2-power body (folded into target 7).Ward {2} trigger on a Sheltered Chocobo or Elves: pay the 2; the blank is only worth 3 mana on Harmonizer/Hydra (target 6).
The Wondrous Wasp
Every creature in this deck is worth exactly its abilities: Hydra and Chocobo are 0/0 and 0/1 bodies that only matter because they grow, Harmonizer only matters for its landfall, Explorer only for its statics. A 2-mana flash 'loses all abilities' is close to a permanent Pacifism on growth against them, and the 2/1 flyer is unblockable in game 1 (no flyers, no reach) for Enduring Curiosity draws. The one thing to internalise: Wasp stops FUTURE triggers only. A landfall trigger already on the stack resolves no matter what you do to its source, and an uncracked fetch land lets them stack a whole second batch above Wasp's trigger, so the best Wasp is cast at their upkeep, before the land drop.
#1Mossborn Hydra
Why'Landfall, double the number of +1/+1 counters on this creature' plus Trample. Blanked, it is frozen at its current counters with no trample; Beavers/Siren chump it forever and Drowner or Kaito -2 lock it. On the draw this is your only play for their T3 Harmonizer turn (you have 2 lands).
WhenBlank: their upkeep or draw step, BEFORE the land drop. If you wait for the landfall batch, the doubling already on the stack resolves regardless, and if an uncracked Fabled Passage/Escape Tunnel is on the battlefield they crack it in response to Wasp's trigger and the second batch (fresh Hydra double, Harmonizer double, Chocobo counter) also resolves before the blank applies. Wasp-in-response only stops the second batch when they have no fetch available. Tap: their beginning of combat step if it would attack for lethal; tapping after attackers are declared does nothing.
AfterStays an X/X where X = its current counters (0/0 base + counters, does not die), loses Trample and Landfall. Erode or Sheltered on Wasp restores it, so kill it while it is blank.
#2Mightform Harmonizer
Why'Landfall, double the power of target creature you control until end of turn.' Blanked, the kill multiplier is gone for every later land. Hardcast (4 mana) Harmonizers are the real target; a warped one leaves at end step anyway and is exiled regardless of the blank (warp's exile is a delayed trigger, not one of its abilities).
WhenTheir upkeep on any turn a Harmonizer is on the battlefield. Against a fresh warp there is no window between the Harmonizer resolving and their land drop (active player gets priority and may play a land immediately), and Wasp cast with the landfall batch on the stack stops nothing that turn if they hold an uncracked fetch (they crack it in response). Accept that against warp-then-fetch the blank applies from their next land onward; on the draw, Wasp the Hydra/Chocobo at upkeep instead so the double has a 1-power target.
AfterVanilla 4/4. Any doubling already resolved this turn stays until end of turn.
#3Icetill Explorer
WhyStrips both statics: 'You may play an additional land on each of your turns' and 'You may play lands from your graveyard', ending the replay-the-fetch loop and the double land drops. No trigger needed, so this is Wasp's job, not Tidebinder's.
WhenAny time; the earlier the better, ideally before their next turn so they never get the extra drop. Lands already played this turn stay played.
AfterVanilla 2/4; landfall mill also gone.
#4Sazh's Chocobo
Why'Landfall, put a +1/+1 counter on this creature.' A 1-drop that is routinely 5/6+ by turn 4 and is the other favourite Harmonizer target. 2 mana to freeze it and remove it as a doubling target is fine value when Hydra is not on board.
WhenTheir upkeep before the land drop; or beginning of combat as a tap to stop a lethal swing.
AfterFrozen at current counters on a 0/1 base, no more growth; a 2/1 flyer trades with nothing here so keep Wasp safe.
#5Dyadrine, Synthesis Amalgam
WhyLoses Trample and 'Whenever you attack, you may remove a +1/+1 counter from each of two creatures you control. If you do, draw a card and create a 2/2 colorless Robot'. Their grind engine becomes a vanilla body with counters; Beavers chump it.
WhenBefore their declare attackers (upkeep or beginning of combat); tap at beginning of combat if the swing matters.
AfterKeeps counters (0/1 base), no trample, no cards, no Robots.
#6Surrak, Elusive Hunter (sideboard)
WhyTargeting Surrak with Wasp draws them one card ('Whenever a creature you control ... becomes the target of a spell or ability an opponent controls, draw a card'), then he is a vanilla 4/3: no more draws off your Drowner/removal/Kaito, no trample. Tidebinder on his first draw trigger is the cleaner answer; Wasp is the fallback.
WhenAny time; sooner is better since each of your targeted plays feeds him.
AfterVanilla 4/3 (his 'can't be countered' was a spell clause and is irrelevant). They draw exactly one from the Wasp targeting.
#7Summon: Esper Maduin (Esper Origins flashed back; 4/4 Saga creature)
WhyEnchantment Creature, Saga Elemental 4/4: Wasp blanks all remaining chapters (no 'Add {G}{G}', no team +2/+2 trample). Under CR 704.5s/714.4 a Saga with no chapter abilities is not sacrificed for its lore counters, so it stays as a blank 4/4 with a finality counter; when Wasp leaves, the chapters return and it is sacrificed (exiled, via the finality counter) if it has 3+ lore counters.
WhenIts ETB chapter I still resolves; Wasp before their next precombat main phase to stop II and III.
AfterAbility-less 4/4; finality counter is not an ability (Scryfall ruling) and still exiles it if it would die.
#8Llanowar Elves
Why'{T}: Add {G}' is a mana ability Tidebinder can't touch but Wasp's blank removes. T1 Elves into T2 3-mana is their fast start. Usually the wrong use: you want Wasp for Hydra/Harmonizer.
WhenTheir T2 upkeep (only possible on the play). Once they are past T3, ignore it.
AfterA 1/1 that taps for nothing.
#9Earthbended land creature
WhyIt is a creature, so Wasp can hit it: loses haste and '{T}: Add {G}', so it produces no mana while Wasp lives (a soft Stone Rain). It stays a creature (the animation is an effect, not an ability) and it STILL returns if it dies: the return is a delayed trigger created by earthbend, not an ability of the land.
WhenAny time; low priority.
AfterA 0/0 with its counters that produces no mana and has no haste; if it dies or is exiled it comes back tapped as normal.
#10Sapling Nursery Treefolk token (sideboard)
WhyA 3/4 reach token is the only thing post-board that blocks Wasp and stops Enduring Curiosity draws. Blanking one token strips reach; only worth it when it is their lone reach body and the Curiosity draws are winning the game.
WhenYour own turn before attacks, or their end step.
AfterVanilla 3/4; Nursery keeps making more, so this is a one-turn-cycle window.
Traps
Any landfall creature: Wasp in response to the landfall trigger to stop it. A triggered ability on the stack exists independently of its source; blanking the source does not counter it. And with an uncracked fetch on the battlefield they crack it in response and the second batch resolves above Wasp's trigger. Wasp before the land (upkeep), or use Tidebinder, which does counter the trigger.
Hydra / Chocobo / Dyadrine: Blanking them kills them because their base is 0/0 or 0/1. +1/+1 counters are not abilities; the creature keeps its size. Only a characteristic-defining P/T ability would be lost, and none of these have one.
Attacking Hydra: Flash Wasp after attackers are declared to tap the attacker and stop damage. Tapping does not remove a creature from combat. Wasp must resolve in their beginning of combat step (or earlier) to prevent the attack. Once declared, Wasp still blanks (no trample) which can matter for damage assignment, but it still hits.
Torpor Orb (sideboard): Wasp still taps and blanks under Torpor Orb. 'Creatures entering don't cause abilities to trigger.' Wasp's Sting never triggers; it is a 2/1 flyer. Flash it while the Orb is on the stack if you must (Wasp, unlike Tidebinder, has a legal target there), or answer the Orb first.
Creature enchanted with Sheltered by Ghosts: Wasp it for 2. It has ward {2}: Wasp's trigger is countered unless you pay 2 more. Wasp's body still enters. Budget 4 mana or pick another target.
Surrak, Elusive Hunter: Free targeting. Every Wasp target while Surrak is out draws them a card. Blank Surrak first (or Tidebinder his first draw trigger).
Warped Mightform Harmonizer: Wasp it to keep it from being exiled and stuck as a blank body, or expect the blank to stop the exile. The warp exile is a delayed trigger created on cast; it fires regardless of the blank. Wasp on a warped Harmonizer only matters for later land drops, and only if they cannot fetch in response.
Earthbended land: Wasp it and then kill it to remove a land for good. The blank removes haste and the mana ability but not the return: 'When it dies or is exiled, return it to the battlefield tapped' is a delayed triggered ability created by earthbend (Scryfall ruling on Ba Sing Se), not an ability of the land. It comes back. Only Tidebinder countering that delayed trigger keeps it dead, and Explorer can replay it from the graveyard anyway.
Day of Black Sun (our sideboard): Sweep with X at 2 or more while Wasp is blanking something. Wasp is MV 2; the sweeper blanks and destroys it, and the moment it dies the blanked Hydra/Harmonizer regains everything (before the destroy resolves on them only if their MV is also X or less). Sweep with X=1 to keep Wasp, or accept losing the blank.
Lines with the rest of the deck
  • On the draw, their T3 is the danger turn and you have 2 lands: Wasp their Hydra (or Chocobo if no Hydra) at their T3 UPKEEP, before the land. Harmonizer then doubles a 1-power body instead of a 4-power one. Tidebinder comes online for their T4.
  • On the play, you have 3 mana on their T3: hold Tidebinder for Harmonizer's landfall trigger. If they have an uncracked Fabled Passage/Escape Tunnel, they will crack it in response and that batch resolves first; if possible wait for the batch from the basic after the crack.
  • Tidebinder counters Hydra's doubling (Hydra blanked, no trample, frozen) -> flash Floodpits Drowner at THEIR end step: its ETB taps the Hydra and puts a stun counter on it -> on your turn, {1}{U},{T}: shuffle Drowner and the Hydra away while the stun counter is still there. Cast Drowner on your own main phase instead and their untap step removes the stun counter before Drowner can tap, so the shuffle has no target; use Kaito -2 (two stun counters) first if Drowner must come down on your turn. The blank stops the doubling and trample; the stun stops the attack.
  • Kaito -2 (tap target creature, two stun counters) on the blanked Hydra or Chocobo locks their only real attacker while Kaito ticks, and leaves one stun counter for Drowner's shuffle on your next turn. Kaito is hexproof on OUR turn, so Sheltered by Ghosts can only target him on theirs: hold Tidebinder for that Aura's ETB post-board.
  • Post-board, if Sheltered by Ghosts lands on Harmonizer or Hydra: target the creature with Kaito -2 / Drowner / Bitter Triumph, let the ward {2} trigger go on the stack, Tidebinder the ward trigger. Your ability resolves untaxed and the creature is blanked (lifelink and ward granted earlier are removed too).
  • Enduring Curiosity + Wasp: game 1 they have zero flyers and zero reach, so a blank-then-swing Wasp draws a card every turn. Post-board Sapling Nursery's 3/4 reach Treefolk stop this unless you Wasp the lone token.
  • Post-board Surrak: the first time Drowner/Wasp/Bitter Triumph/Shoot the Sheriff/Kaito -2 targets their creature, Surrak's draw trigger goes on the stack; Tidebinder it and Surrak is blank for the rest of the game while your spell still resolves.
  • Protect the blank rather than relying on it: their outs are Erode (4 main; Erode's basic-land search goes to the destroyed permanent's controller, so Eroding our Tidebinder gives US the land, but it still ends the blank) and Sheltered by Ghosts (4 side). Spell Pierce and We Say Thee Nay! both counter Erode and Sheltered unless they pay {2}, so with a blank on board hold the soft counter for the Erode. Kill the blanked threat with Bitter Triumph/Shoot the Sheriff/Drowner while it is blank.
  • Post-board Torpor Orb: answer it (Annul, Duress takes it as a noncreature nonland card, Spell Snare hits MV 2). Only Wasp gains anything from being flashed in while the Orb is on the stack (it taps/blanks a creature); Tidebinder in that window has no ability to counter. Under a resolved Orb both are bodies only.
  • Their T3 on the play (their T4 on the draw) with Tidebinder in hand and nothing else to do on their turn: counter a fetch-land crack. The land is already sacrificed, no basic arrives, no second landfall batch. A 3-mana Stone Rain plus a 3/2; Explorer can replay the fetch later, so it buys a land drop, not a land.
  • Day of Black Sun: choose X with your own blankers in mind. X=1 kills Chocobo, Elves and earthbended lands (MV 0-1) and keeps Wasp (MV 2) and Tidebinder (MV 3); X=2 also kills Hydra but destroys Wasp and ends its blank; X=3 destroys Tidebinder too. Their Sapling Nursery indestructible response never matters (the sweeper removes all abilities before destroying), so do not answer it.
Hold or fire
Tidebinder: HOLD. Never main-phase it as a 3/2 before turn 6 in this matchup; a 3/2 does not block tramplers and every one of their real plays is a trigger you will want to counter. Fire on, in order: (1) a Harmonizer power-double that would connect for lethal or 6+; (2) a Hydra doubling from 2+ counters when you can survive the turn (permanent cap); (3) Explorer's mill once they are replaying fetches from the graveyard; (4) post-board, Sheltered by Ghosts' ETB aimed at Kaito, Curiosity or your blanker, the ward trigger on a Sheltered Harmonizer/Hydra, and Surrak's first draw trigger. Fire it as a fetch-crack Stone Rain (their T3 on the play, T4 on the draw) only if you are otherwise wasting the mana on their turn. Do not flash it in response to Torpor Orb; it has nothing to counter there. Wasp: FIRE EARLY. Its best moment is their upkeep on the growth creature (Hydra > Harmonizer > Explorer > Chocobo) before the land drop; waiting for the perfect moment lets a trigger already on the stack through, and an uncracked fetch lets them stack a second batch above Wasp's trigger. On the play: Tidebinder is live for their T3, so Wasp can wait for a hardcast Harmonizer or Explorer. On the draw: you cannot Tidebinder their T3, so Wasp the doubling target at their T3 upkeep and hold Tidebinder for T4. Never blank and forget: schedule the removal for the blanked threat, hold Spell Pierce / We Say Thee Nay! for the Erode, and set Day of Black Sun's X below your blankers' mana value if the blank is still doing work.
How many to run
Tidebinder: 1 main (stock) is right for game 1; bring in both sideboard copies for games 2-3 (3 total). This deck's entire plan is triggered abilities on creatures, so the blank rider almost always applies, and post-board Sheltered by Ghosts (ETB and ward trigger) and Surrak add three more premium targets. Wasp: keep both (2), do not want more than the 75 offers; against 4 Erode plus 4 Sheltered a blank is a window, not an answer, so pair the five blankers with your hard removal and with Spell Pierce (2 side, counters Erode and Sheltered unless they pay {2}) rather than adding a sixth blanker. Post-board also keep Annul in for Torpor Orb, which switches off all five ETBs, and remember Day of Black Sun at X of 2 or more destroys your own Wasp/Tidebinder and ends their blanks.
Review trail: confidence high, 7 disputed, 17 changes
Disputed reviewer points (kept as written)
  • Reviewer 2 (Drowner line): 'Drowner's shuffle ability requires {T} and sorcery timing'. Rejected in part: Floodpits Drowner's oracle is '{1}{U}, {T}: Shuffle this creature and target creature with a stun counter on it into their owners' libraries.' with no timing restriction, so it can be activated at instant speed. Accepted the underlying point that a Drowner cast on our own main phase cannot self-loop (their untap step removes the single stun counter before Drowner can tap); the line now specifies flashing Drowner at their end step or pairing with Kaito -2.
  • Reviewer 1 (Drowner line): 'one turn, not two' accepted; Reviewer 1's implication that the stun counter alone carries the timing is correct only when Drowner is flashed on their turn (see above).
  • Reviewer 2 (earthbend delayed trigger): 'source is then a card in the graveyard/exile, nothing is blanked'. Right outcome, wrong reason: under CR 603.7 the source of a delayed triggered ability is the source of the spell or ability that created it, i.e. Ba Sing Se (land) or Earthbender Ascension (enchantment). Reviewer 1's version is the one used. Nothing is blanked either way.
  • Reviewer 2 (Day of Black Sun): 'verify Day of Black Sun's oracle; keep the line only if it destroys creatures'. Verified from the data set: 'Each creature with mana value X or less loses all abilities until end of turn. Destroy those creatures.' It does destroy, but the line is removed anyway because the sweeper strips indestructible itself (Reviewer 1), so the Nursery response is never worth answering.
  • Retracted from my own previous 'disputed' list: I had claimed the hint block was wrong to attribute the earthbend return clause to Ba Sing Se rather than to the earthbended land. The Scryfall ruling on Ba Sing Se ('the delayed triggered ability created by earthbend') and CR 603.7 show the hint block was closer to right: the return is a delayed trigger whose source is the earthbend source, not an ability of the land.
  • Kept from the previous round (unchanged, oracle-backed): the file's regex hint block lists Landfall only as a keyword and omits the landfall triggers on Mightform Harmonizer, Mossborn Hydra, Sazh's Chocobo, Icetill Explorer and Sapling Nursery; it omits Summon: Esper Maduin's chapter abilities (chapter II is confirmed not a mana ability by Scryfall ruling); it omits the ward {2} trigger granted by Sheltered by Ghosts.
  • Kept: the CR 704.5s/714.4 claim that a Saga with no chapter abilities is not sacrificed for its lore counters. Both reviewers accepted it; the 'flag to double-check' hedge is dropped, with the caveat that lore counters still accumulate (a Saga rule, not an ability) so the sacrifice happens as soon as the blank ends with 3+ counters.

vs. Azorius Momo2.5% TB: situationalWasp: good▼
A one-drop flier tempo deck built around Momo, Friendly Flier ({W}, "The first non-Lemur creature spell with flying you cast during each of your turns costs {1} less to cast") and Springleaf Drum. Turn 1 is Momo or Drum; turn 2 is where the deck explodes, because Momo's discount also applies to warp costs: Quantum Riddler warps for {U} and Starfield Shepherd for {W} while Momo is out. Their two nut lines are both turn-2 plays: (a) warp Riddler for {U}, then Daydream ({W}, "Exile target creature you control, then return that card to the battlefield ... with a +1/+1 counter") so the warp end-step exile never finds the new object and they keep a permanent 5/7 flier that draws an extra card whenever they draw with one or fewer cards in hand; (b) Drum, then a discounted Sage of the Skies as the second spell so its cast trigger copies it (two 2/3 flying lifelinkers). Daydream (4 copies, flashback {2}{W}) also re-fires every ETB and resets any stun counter or Wasp/Tidebinder blank. Practiced Offense (+1/+1 counter on the team plus double strike or lifelink) with Stirring Hopesinger's Repartee stacking more counters closes games in the air by turn 4-6. Glen Elendra Guardian counters one noncreature spell, and Erode ({W}, 3 main + 1 side) kills our blockers, our Kaito, and any Tidebinder or Wasp whose blank is holding their board down.
Tishana's Tidebinder
Real targets exist but they are narrow windows, and the deck's best turns happen before we have three lands. Most of their engine is untouchable by Tidebinder: Momo's discount is static, Daydream / Practiced Offense / Erode are spells, Springleaf Drum is a mana ability, and warp bodies leave at end step anyway. What Tidebinder does uniquely well here, in order: (1) counter the ETB draw of a Quantum Riddler that is STAYING (Daydream re-entry or hard cast) so the deck's namesake 5/7 loses flying and its card engine; (2) counter Glen Elendra Guardian's activation so our removal resolves and Guardian is disarmed while Tidebinder lives; (3) counter Sage of the Skies' copy trigger; (4) counter a Repartee trigger to shut off Hopesinger. Everything else is a 3-mana answer to a 1-2 mana play. Timing reality: on the play we have {2}{U} during their turn 3 at the earliest, on the draw their turn 4, so the FIRST Riddler + Daydream and the turn-2 Sage copy are not Tidebinder targets; the flashback Daydream, the hard-cast Riddler, and later Sage/Guardian windows are. The 3/2 ground body is poor against an all-flier board and Erode kills it for {W} (ending every blank), so treat Tidebinder as a scalpel and keep it at one copy.
#1Quantum Riddler (only one that is staying: Daydream re-entry or a 5-mana hard cast)
When this creature enters, draw a card.
WhyA permanent Riddler is what this deck is named for: a 4/6 (5/7 after Daydream) flier plus the static replacement 'As long as you have one or fewer cards in hand, if you would draw one or more cards, you draw that many cards plus one instead.' Countering the ETB is Tidebinder's only hook into that static text and its flying. Afterward it is a ground wall our Dream Beavers, Spyglass Siren and Wasp fly over, and Enduring Curiosity or Tidebinder itself can block it.
WhenAfter Daydream resolves (Riddler re-enters with a +1/+1 counter and its draw trigger goes on the stack) or after a hard cast resolves: flash Tidebinder targeting the draw trigger. Realistic windows are their turn 3+ on the play / turn 4+ on the draw, which in practice means the flashback Daydream ({2}{W}) or a hard cast; the first warp-plus-Daydream Riddler on their turn 2 is a Wasp job. Do NOT do this to a warp-cast Riddler that has not been Daydreamed (see traps).
AfterLoses: flying, the ETB, the extra-draw replacement effect. Keeps: 4/6 or 5/7 body and its counters, and any double strike / lifelink Practiced Offense grants it afterwards (an ability gained after the effect began is kept, ruling 2).
RiskAnother Daydream (or Morningtide's Light from the side) makes it a new object and restores everything. Erode / Get Lost on Tidebinder restores it instantly. Floodpits Drowner is the cleaner answer when available: its own ETB stuns the Riddler, then {1}{U},{T} shuffles it away (the shuffle needs the stun counter; a Wasp or Tidebinder blank does not enable it).
#2Glen Elendra Guardian
{1}{U}, Remove a counter from this creature: Counter target noncreature spell. Its controller draws a card.
WhyGuardian is the card that protects their permanent Riddler from Bitter Triumph and Shoot the Sheriff and stops our counterspells, Kaito hard-casts, Duress and Day of Black Sun (it cannot touch creature spells, so Wasp, Drowner and Enduring Curiosity are safe). Tidebinder on the activation means our spell resolves, they lose the {1}{U} and the counter they paid, and Guardian is a vanilla 3/4 with no flying while Tidebinder lives. Note 'Its controller draws a card' refers to the countered SPELL's controller, i.e. us: countering Guardian's ability also costs us that consolation card, a small price for the spell resolving.
WhenReactive, on our turn. Cast the removal with {2}{U} still open; they activate Guardian (the -1/-1 counter comes off as a cost, so Guardian is now 3/4); flash Tidebinder targeting the activation. Tidebinder's trigger resolves first, the activation is countered, our spell resolves. Check the counters first: a Guardian with NO counter (Daydreamed, or pumped by Practiced Offense / Repartee / Air Temple while its -1/-1 was on) cannot activate at all and needs no answer. Cheaper alternative: bait the single counter with the spell you care least about (Requiting Hex on Momo, a Duress) and then cast the real removal.
AfterLoses: flying, flash, the counter ability, for as long as Tidebinder is on the battlefield. Keeps: 3/4 body. The spent counter stays spent, but 'Remove a counter' means ANY counter, so once Tidebinder is gone a +1/+1 counter from Practiced Offense, Repartee or Abandoned Air Temple re-arms it.
RiskErode or Get Lost on Tidebinder returns Guardian's abilities (not the spent counter). Their single Spider-Sense can counter Tidebinder's trigger. Flash-Guardian-then-activate needs 5 open mana on our turn, so from hand it is only a late-game threat.
#3Sage of the Skies
When you cast this spell, if you've cast another spell this turn, copy this spell. (The copy becomes a token.)
WhyThe copy is the deck's main two-for-one: a second 2/3 flying lifelink body that also pumps Momo. Countering the trigger takes a 3-mana token off the table for our 3 mana plus we get a 3/2 body, so it is card and mana parity on the turn they went all-in.
WhenTheir main phase, after they have already cast a spell this turn (intervening if: the trigger only goes on the stack if they cast something earlier). Sage on the stack, copy trigger above it, flash Tidebinder targeting the trigger. Their fastest copy is turn 2 (Momo, then Drum + discounted Sage) and we cannot answer that; the realistic window is their turn 3+ on the play, 4+ on the draw, e.g. warp Shepherd for {W} then Sage.
AfterThe trigger's source is the Sage SPELL on the stack, not a permanent, so Sage itself loses nothing (ruling 3): it resolves as a normal 2/3 flying lifelink and Momo still gets +1/+1. You only deny the copy.
RiskOnly worth it when the copy is live; a Sage cast as their first spell of the turn has no trigger to counter.
#4Stirring Hopesinger
Repartee, Whenever you cast an instant or sorcery spell that targets a creature, put a +1/+1 counter on each creature you control.
WhyEvery Daydream, Practiced Offense, Morningtide's Light, and any Erode / Get Lost aimed at a creature becomes a team pump. Counter one instance and Hopesinger is a 1/3 ground body with no lifelink and no Repartee while Tidebinder lives, which also fixes the racing math.
WhenTheir turn (Daydream / Offense are sorceries) or in response to Erode / Get Lost on one of our creatures: the spell goes on the stack, Repartee triggers above it, flash Tidebinder targeting the Repartee trigger. The spell itself still resolves. Erode on Kaito while he is a planeswalker (their turn) does not target a creature and does not trigger Repartee. With two Hopesingers out you counter one trigger and the other still pumps.
AfterLoses: flying, lifelink, Repartee. Keeps: 1/3 body and every +1/+1 counter already on it. Counters already placed on the rest of the team from earlier triggers stay.
RiskDaydream or Morningtide's Light on Hopesinger makes it a new object and restores everything.
#5Steamcore Scholar
When this creature enters, draw two cards. Then discard two cards unless you discard an instant or sorcery card or a creature card with flying.
WhyDenies a 2-card dig and leaves a 2/2 ground body with no vigilance. Fine when the window is free, not worth holding mana for (2 copies).
WhenTheir main phase, ETB on the stack, flash Tidebinder targeting it.
AfterLoses flying, vigilance, the ETB. Keeps the 2/2 body.
RiskLow value; Daydream resets.
#6Momo, Friendly Flier
Whenever another creature you control with flying enters, Momo gets +1/+1 until end of turn.
WhyThe pump itself is trivial; the point is that countering it is the only way Tidebinder can switch off Momo's static discount, which is otherwise untouchable. Requiting Hex ({B}, MV 2 or less) answers Momo permanently and has almost no other target in their deck, so only do this when Hex is not in hand and you wanted the 3/2 body anyway.
WhenTheir turn, when a flier of theirs enters and Momo's trigger is on the stack; flash Tidebinder targeting it.
AfterLoses: flying, the discount, the pump trigger. Keeps: 1/1 body.
RiskSpending 3 mana on a 1-mana creature that Hex kills outright.
#7Flitterwing Nuisance
{2}{U}, Remove a counter from this creature: Whenever a creature you control deals combat damage to a player or planeswalker this turn, draw a card.
WhyOne copy. Countering the activation denies every draw for the turn, and they have already spent the mana and the counter; Nuisance is left a 2/2 with no flying. Alternative: let the activation resolve and counter one of the resulting 'deals combat damage' draw triggers after damage; its source is still Nuisance (a delayed trigger created by an ability shares that ability's source), so Nuisance is blanked either way, but you deny only one draw instead of all of them.
WhenTheir precombat main when they activate; flash Tidebinder targeting the activation.
AfterLoses flying and the activated ability. Keeps 2/2 body (counter already removed as cost). Note Shoot the Sheriff cannot kill it (Faerie Rogue = outlaw); Requiting Hex can (MV 1).
RiskLow priority; only if you have nothing else for the mana.
#8Abandoned Air Temple
{3}{W}, {T}: Put a +1/+1 counter on each creature you control.
WhyCounterable, and 4 of their mana for 3 of ours is a fine trade late, but the source is a land so nothing loses abilities. Only when the pump would kill our blockers or swing a race. Side note: if a Guardian or Nuisance is still holding its -1/-1 counter, this pump (like Practiced Offense or Repartee) annihilates it and DISARMS that creature, so sometimes you want it to resolve.
WhenWhenever they activate (usually precombat on their turn): flash Tidebinder targeting the activation. The {3}{W} and the tap stay paid.
AfterNothing changes on the land; it can activate again next turn.
RiskYou spent your Tidebinder on a land.
Traps
Momo, Friendly Flier: Counter the cost reduction when they cast a cheap flier or warp a Riddler for {U}. 'costs {1} less to cast' is a static ability; nothing goes on the stack. The only counterable thing on Momo is the +1/+1 trigger (target 6). Wasp removes the discount; Tidebinder cannot except via that trigger.
Springleaf Drum: Counter the Drum activation to strand their turn-2 play. '{T}, Tap an untapped creature you control: Add one mana of any color' is a mana ability; Tidebinder's own reminder text says mana abilities can't be targeted. Wasp tapping their only creature at upkeep is the way to turn Drum off (it needs an untapped creature).
