RiskLow; you deny 3 mana and two temporary tokens plus six engine triggers, which is a fair use if Took is not on board.
#7Captain Marvel, Earth's Protector (sideboard, 1 copy)
Power-up, {5}{W}{W}: Put a +1/+1 counter and an indestructible counter on Captain Marvel. (Activate each power-up ability only once. Reduce the cost by her mana cost if she entered this turn.)
WhyFootnote: 7 mana in one turn (5 flash + 2 discounted power-up) from a 22-land deck, one sideboard copy. If it happens, it happens at our end step, so we can respond: no counters, and because she is a creature she loses flying, lifelink and the power-up while Tidebinder lives. 'Activate only once' counts the countered activation, so she never powers up again.
WhenIn response to the same-turn discounted power-up, usually our end step. If she waits to power up on their turn under Voice you cannot respond.
AfterA vanilla 5/4 (6/5 with Call), no flying, no lifelink, no counters ever; Restless Reef's deathtouch Shark or a chump blocker handles her.
RiskTidebinder dying only gives back flying/lifelink; the counters are gone for good.
#8Fountainport (card draw)
{2}, {T}, Sacrifice a token: Draw a card.
WhyOnly when Tidebinder has nothing better to do at your end step: they sacrifice a token and pay {2} plus tap for nothing. Land source, no blank. Also note the same land's Fish and Treasure activations at your end step create tokens, which is a Took-trigger (and, for the Fish, Call/Triumph) window on YOUR turn; the Treasure only triggers Took.
WhenYour end step, in response to the activation. The token is already sacrificed (cost).
AfterNo card drawn; token stays gone; Fountainport unchanged.
RiskYou spent your only Tidebinder to deny one card.
#9Warleader's Call (ping)
Whenever a creature you control enters, this enchantment deals 1 damage to each opponent.
WhyOnly when that single point is lethal or sets up an exact race. Enchantment source, nothing blanked, every other token's ping still resolves.
WhenAny creature entry you can respond to.
AfterOne point prevented; Call keeps its anthem and future pings.
RiskAlmost always a waste of the card.
Traps
Voice of Victory: Flash in Tidebinder during their attack to counter Voice's Mobilize 2 and blank Voice. This window never exists. Voice's Mobilize only triggers when Voice attacks, which happens on its controller's turn with that Voice un-blanked, and its static 'Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn' then forbids casting Tidebinder. A Voice already blanked by Wasp has no Mobilize; a stunned Voice cannot attack; a second, un-blanked Voice attacking locks you out too. The only 'Mobilize tokens' Tidebinder can ever respond to are Stadium Headliner's and Dalkovan Encampment's. The lockout ends only via Wasp, Day of Black Sun, or Voice leaving the battlefield (Requiting Hex, Shoot the Sheriff, Bitter Triumph, Drowner's shuffle).
Voice of Victory / Stadium Headliner / Dalkovan Encampment Warriors: Counter the 'Sacrifice them at the beginning of the next end step' delayed trigger on the Mobilize tokens. It is a legal target and, because a delayed trigger's source is the creature whose Mobilize created it (CR 603.7d), countering Headliner's version at their end step would blank Headliner. But they KEEP the Warriors permanently, the window only exists with no un-blanked Voice out, and Voice's own version is unreachable (Voice attacked un-blanked that turn, so the lockout was live). Encampment's version has a land as its source and blanks nothing. Bad trade; do not target it.
Warleader's Call: Counter the +1/+1 anthem. 'Creatures you control get +1/+1' is a static ability; only the 1-damage trigger can be targeted, and Call is an enchantment so it never loses abilities.
Song of Totentanz / Hop to It / Frontline Rush / The Last Ronin's Technique: Tidebinder the token creation or the haste. These are spells resolving, not abilities. 'Creatures you control gain haste' and 'they enter tapped and attacking' are spell effects; Sneak is an alternative cost paid while casting. Answer them with We Say Thee Nay!, Spell Snare (Frontline Rush is MV 2), Spell Pierce or Flashfreeze, on your turn if Voice is out, on theirs if it is not.
Stadium Headliner: Countering the sac-ping 'locks' Headliner. Sacrifice is a cost, so Headliner is in the graveyard when Tidebinder's trigger resolves; ruling 3 says a source that is not an artifact/creature/planeswalker at resolution loses nothing. You counter the damage only. To blank Headliner, counter its Mobilize 1 while it is attacking.
Political Triumph / Warleader's Call / Rest in Peace / Fountainport / Dalkovan Encampment: Countering one of their triggers or activations shuts the permanent off. Enchantments and lands are not artifacts, creatures or planeswalkers; the ability is countered but the permanent keeps every ability. Only Took, Queen, Marvel and Headliner (via Mobilize 1) can be blanked in this 75.
Sacred Foundry / Inspiring Vantage / Dalkovan Encampment: Respond to 'As this land enters, you may pay 2 life' or 'enters tapped unless'. Replacement effects and static abilities; they never use the stack.
Torch the Tower: Counter the Bargain sacrifice or the 'exile it instead' clause. Bargain is part of casting; the exile clause is a replacement effect created by the resolving spell. Neither is an ability on the stack.
Fountainport: '{4}, {T}: Create a Treasure token' is a mana ability and cannot be targeted. It is NOT a mana ability (creating a Treasure does not add mana), so it CAN be targeted, but it is worthless to counter. The '{T}: Add {C}' line and sacrificing the Treasure itself are the mana abilities.
Dawn's Truce (sideboard): Their hexproof instant fizzles Tidebinder's trigger. Tidebinder targets an ability on the stack, not a permanent; 'you and permanents you control gain hexproof' does not protect abilities. Dawn's Truce does fizzle Wasp's Sting.
Kaito, Bane of Nightmares (ours): Kaito's −2 ('Tap target creature. Put two stun counters on it') on Voice of Victory turns off the lockout. Static abilities work while tapped or stunned; the lockout stays. The play is still good for other reasons: Voice cannot attack through their next two untap steps (no Mobilize 2, no Warriors, no engine triggers) and the second stun counter is what lets Floodpits Drowner shuffle Voice away on your following turn.
Not worth it
Warleader's Call ping trigger: 1 damage per counter, nothing blanked; only when it is the exact lethal point.Dalkovan Encampment's {2}{W},{T} activation in their main phase: let it resolve and counter the 'Whenever you attack this turn' delayed trigger in declare attackers instead (same result, their mana already spent). Both need no un-blanked Voice.Fountainport Fish ('{3}, {T}, Pay 1 life: Create a 1/1 blue Fish') and Treasure activations: legal targets, worthless; the token entering is more useful as the Took-trigger window on your turn.Rest in Peace ETB ('When this enchantment enters, exile all graveyards'): counterable but pointless. The part that hurts us is the static replacement effect (it exiles Enduring Curiosity instead of letting it die and return), and that is not an ability on the stack; an enchantment never blanks. Answer RIP with Annul on the stack or Get-Lost-style removal, not Tidebinder. They rarely board it against Dimir.Captain Marvel's flash, flying and lifelink are keyword abilities, not stack objects; only the power-up is a target.Get Lost (their sideboard) hands US two Map tokens; they are our artifacts with a sorcery-speed activated ability, not targets for either card, listed so the pilot is not surprised that artifacts appear.
The Wondrous Wasp
Wasp does two things here: it blanks Voice of Victory's lockout ('Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn') on our own main phase, reopening our instant-speed game for their turn, and it blanks Belladonna Took or (post-board) The Queen of Dale without needing a trigger window. It is a suspension, not removal: their 75 has 3 Torch the Tower, 4 Erode and 2 Get Lost for a 2/1, and Requiting Hex (1 mana, permanent, no Torch window) kills every one of those creatures. So Hex is the default answer to Voice and Took; Wasp is the answer when Hex is committed elsewhere, when they are tapped out, or as the second copy. The 2/1 flyer also connects every turn against an all-ground main deck for Enduring Curiosity. The 'tap before attackers' mode is mostly unavailable: Voice stops us casting Wasp on their turn, and the only pre-attack tap target worth it (Captain Marvel) is behind the same lockout.
#1Voice of Victory
WhyBlanks both 'Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn' and Mobilize 2. With the static gone we can flash Tidebinder into Hop to It / Song / Headliner-Mobilize tokens, We Say Thee Nay! a Song of Totentanz, and kill things at instant speed on their turn. Prefer Requiting Hex on Voice when they have red open: if they Torch Wasp in response to Wasp's Sting, Wasp is gone before the Sting resolves, the 'for as long as Wasp remains' duration has already ended, and Voice is never blanked (the tap still happens). Wasp Voice when they are tapped out, or when Hex is needed for Took.
WhenOUR turn, main phase 1 (we cannot cast it on theirs while an un-blanked Voice is out). Cast it before anything else that turn. On the draw their Voice lands turn 2: hold Spell Snare on turn 1 if you have it (Voice is MV 2, the lockout does not exist while it is a spell); otherwise Hex or Wasp on our turn 2/3 main. On the play: Spell Snare / We Say Thee Nay! on the Voice spell turn 2, or Hex/Wasp turn 3.
AfterVoice is a 1/3 (2/4 with Call) with no text while Wasp lives; a second Voice still locks us out. Tokens already created stay. If Wasp dies the lockout returns the instant the removal resolves, but while Torch the Tower is on the stack on their turn, Voice is still blank, so respond with your instants right then.
#2Belladonna Took
WhyBlanks the token trigger ('Whenever a token you control enters, you gain 1 life ... draw a card ... put a +1/+1 counter on each creature you control') without needing a trigger window, which Tidebinder does need. If Voice is not on the battlefield and Hex is not in hand, Took is the target.
WhenOur turn (or their end step if no un-blanked Voice is out). Do it before their next token spell, not after.
After2/2 (3/3 with Call) body with no text while Wasp lives; counters already on their team stay, cards already drawn stay drawn.
#3The Queen of Dale (sideboard)
WhyBlanks 'Whenever an opponent casts their first noncreature spell each turn, you recruit', so our removal and counters stop feeding them loots and Soldier tokens.
WhenOur main phase BEFORE casting the turn's first noncreature spell. Wasp is a creature spell and does not trigger her.
After2/1 (3/2 with Call) vanilla; she does not fly, so Wasp blocks her.
#4Captain Marvel, Earth's Protector (sideboard, 1 copy)
WhyRemoves flying and lifelink (and the power-up if unused), turning a 5/4 flying lifelinker into a ground body that Restless Reef's deathtouch Shark or a chump can handle. Only a blanked Marvel can be blocked by the Shark (she flies otherwise).
WhenTap-before-attackers only in their beginning of combat with no un-blanked Voice out; otherwise on our turn purely for the blank. Footnote, medium confidence: an indestructible counter she already carries from a resolved power-up has the timestamp of when it was placed, so a later 'loses all abilities' takes that keyword away while Wasp lives; the counter itself stays and works again once Wasp leaves; a counter placed after Wasp's effect began is kept (ruling 2).
After5/4 (6/5 with Call) with no abilities while Wasp lives. She can still be powered up after Wasp dies if the ability was never activated.
#5Stadium Headliner
WhyBlanks '{1}{R}, Sacrifice this creature: It deals damage equal to the number of creatures you control to target creature' and Mobilize 1, protecting Enduring Curiosity / Beavers / Wasp itself from their only creature-based removal.
WhenOur turn, when nothing better is on their board (no Voice, no Took, no Queen).
After1/1 (2/2 with Call) that can still attack and block but cannot snipe or Mobilize.
Traps
Any Mobilize or Song of Totentanz attacker: Flash Wasp in during their attack to tap the attacker. Voice's static stops you casting Wasp on their turn at all, and even without Voice, tapping a creature after attackers are declared does not remove it from combat. Tap-before-attackers only works in their beginning of combat step.
Voice of Victory into open red mana: Wasp on Voice is safe whenever it is our turn. If they Torch the Tower (or Erode) Wasp in response to Wasp's Sting, Wasp has left before the Sting resolves; the 'for as long as Wasp remains on the battlefield' duration is already over, so the tap happens but Voice never loses its abilities and the lockout never lifts. Use Requiting Hex / Shoot the Sheriff on Voice into open red; Wasp it when they are tapped out.
Rat tokens from Song of Totentanz: Blank a Rat to shrink their board. Its only ability is 'This token can't block'; removing it lets the Rat block your Kaito or Curiosity.
Dawn's Truce (sideboard): Wasp's Sting is guaranteed once Wasp is cast. 'You and permanents you control gain hexproof until end of turn' cast in response makes the targeted creature an illegal target and the Sting fizzles; Wasp still enters as a 2/1 flyer. They can cast it on our turn with {1}{W} open. No native hexproof or ward in their 75 otherwise.
Voice of Victory: Once Voice is blanked the lockout is gone for the game. It returns the moment Wasp leaves (Torch, Erode, Get Lost, a chump block) and a second Voice is unaffected. Use the window that turn; if they Torch Wasp on their turn, cast your instants while Torch is still on the stack.
Kaito ninjutsu returning Wasp: Free value: ninjutsu Kaito off an unblocked Wasp and replay Wasp for a second Sting. Mechanically fine, but returning Wasp to hand ends the blank on Voice/Took immediately. Only do it if the Wasp target is already dead or you are replaying Wasp onto a better target this turn.
Lines with the rest of the deck
  • Default answer, not a Wasp line: Requiting Hex on Voice of Victory or Belladonna Took on our own turn. Every creature in their main deck is MV 2 or less (Voice 2, Took 2, Headliner 1; Queen 2 post-board). Hex is permanent, 1 mana, and cannot be undone by Torch the way a Wasp blank can. Spend Wasp when Hex is committed or they are tapped out.
  • THE Wasp line: our main phase, Wasp on Voice of Victory while they have no red open. Their turn is now open: hold We Say Thee Nay! (Teamwork: tap Wasp alone, power 2, for the {4} tax; Wasp stays on the battlefield so the blank holds) for Song of Totentanz or Hop to It; flash Tidebinder when Hop / Song / Headliner-Mobilize tokens enter to counter Took's trigger and blank her; Bitter Triumph or Shoot the Sheriff on Took at instant speed. If they Torch Wasp on their upkeep, respond while Torch is on the stack: Voice is still blank until it resolves.
  • Permanent fix for Voice with Floodpits Drowner, the sequencing matters: Drowner cast on OUR turn cannot shuffle that Voice on its own. Drowner's {T} ability is summoning-sick through their turn, and their untap step removes the single stun counter (Voice stays tapped that turn but has no counter), so on our next turn there is no legal target. Two lines that do work: (1) Wasp blanks Voice on our turn, then flash Drowner at THEIR end step onto Voice, then shuffle at our upkeep (Drowner has been ours since our turn began, Voice still has the counter). (2) Kaito's −2 on Voice (two stun counters) with a Drowner already on the battlefield from an earlier turn: their untap eats one counter, Voice cannot attack, and on our next turn Drowner shuffles Voice using the second counter for {1}{U}.
  • Stop the first Voice before it exists: Spell Snare (MV 2) or We Say Thee Nay! on the Voice spell on their turn 2, including on the draw with one untapped Island. The restriction is a static on the battlefield and does nothing while Voice is a spell. Spell Snare also hits Belladonna Took, The Queen of Dale, Frontline Rush, Rest in Peace, Get Lost and Dawn's Truce.
  • Queen of Dale post-board: Wasp or Hex her precombat, THEN cast Bitter Triumph / Shoot the Sheriff freely. Without either: cast the removal, let her recruit trigger go on the stack, flash Tidebinder to counter it and blank her.
  • Wasp + Enduring Curiosity: nothing in their main deck flies, so the 2/1 Wasp hits every turn and 'Whenever a creature you control deals combat damage to a player, draw a card' triggers. Ninjutsu Kaito off the unblocked Wasp only if the Wasp blank no longer matters.
  • Tidebinder as a bodyguard: when Stadium Headliner is sacrificed at Enduring Curiosity or Dream Beavers on our turn, flash Tidebinder and counter the damage; Headliner is already gone, our creature lives, and we have a 3/2 blocker for the anthem'd tokens. This is the Tidebinder play that actually recurs in the matchup.
  • Token-entry windows on OUR turn are the other Tidebinder chances: Frontline Rush cast at instant speed (two Goblins = Took x2, Call x2, Triumph x2), or a Fountainport Fish or Treasure at our end step (Treasure triggers only Took). Prioritise Took's trigger; if Hex is in hand, Hex Took in response to Rush instead and keep Tidebinder's mana for Kaito or Curiosity.
  • Sideboard sweeper: Day of Black Sun for X=2 ('Each creature with mana value X or less loses all abilities until end of turn. Destroy those creatures') kills every token, Voice, Took, Headliner and Queen on our turn, immune to the lockout. It is symmetrical: our Wasp (2), Floodpits Drowner (2), Dream Beavers (1), Spyglass Siren (1) and Wan Shi Tong (MV 2 on the battlefield) die too; Tidebinder (3), Enduring Curiosity (4) and Kaito (4) survive. Cast it when their board is worth more than ours.
  • What still works on their turn under Voice: Voice restricts casting SPELLS only, so activated abilities are legal. Restless Reef's '{2}{U}{B}: becomes a 4/4 Shark with deathtouch' gives a deathtouch blocker for anthem'd ground attackers or a Frontline-Rush-pumped Took (it cannot block an un-blanked Captain Marvel, she flies); Soulstone Sanctuary animates; Drowner's shuffle works if Drowner is unsick and the target still has a stun counter; Ghost Vacuum exiles. Kaito's loyalty abilities are sorcery-speed and Map tokens are sorcery-speed, so those are our-turn only regardless.
Hold or fire
WASP: on your own turn, at the first Voice of Victory, choose by their mana. Red open and Requiting Hex / Shoot the Sheriff in hand: kill Voice with the removal spell (a Torch in response to Wasp's Sting means the blank never happens). They are tapped out, or Hex is needed for Took: Wasp the Voice, cast it before anything else that turn, then hold your instants for their turn. No Voice on board: Wasp Took (or Hex her). Post-board: Wasp or Hex Queen of Dale before your first noncreature spell. Never hold Wasp for a their-turn combat tap in this matchup: Voice makes that mode unavailable, and when there is no Voice the only creature worth tapping pre-attack is Captain Marvel, who is usually protected by the same lockout. From turn 4 with none of those targets on board, flash Wasp at the end of their turn (only if no un-blanked Voice) as a Curiosity clock rather than sitting on it. TIDEBINDER: never hold it for a their-turn trigger while an un-blanked Voice is on the battlefield; you cannot cast it then. Never hold 3 mana for it on your own turn ahead of Kaito or Curiosity; it is the spare-mana play (turn 5 with Kaito already down and 3 left). Its live windows on your turn are reactive: Headliner sac-ping at your creature, Queen's recruit trigger, Frontline Rush / Fountainport token entering (Took trigger, or Triumph's fourth counter if it happens to line up), Marvel's same-turn power-up. If none of those has appeared by turn 4-5, cast it in your main phase as a 3/2 with no target; blocking a 2/2 anthem token is fine. Once Voice is blanked or gone, Tidebinder becomes a real card: their turn, Took's trigger off Hop to It / Song / Headliner tokens, or Headliner's Mobilize 1 itself if the sac-ping is the threat. ON THE DRAW: their turn-2 Voice comes when you have one land; hold Spell Snare on turn 1 if you have it; otherwise plan Hex or Wasp for your turn 2/3 main. ON THE PLAY: Spell Snare / We Say Thee Nay! on the Voice spell itself turn 2 is the cleanest answer; hold that counter.
How many to run
Wasp: keep both main copies and do not side them out. It is a good, not premium, card here: it blanks Voice's lockout and Took on our turn, blanks Queen post-board, and flies over an all-ground board for Curiosity, but it is a 2/1 suspension into 3 Torch, 4 Erode and 2 Get Lost, and Requiting Hex does the removal job permanently for 1 mana. Tidebinder: keep the 1 main copy (it always has a job: Headliner's sac-ping, Queen's recruit, a 3/2 body) but do NOT bring in the 2 sideboard copies. The deck's real triggers (Took, Triumph, Mobilize) fire on their turn behind Voice, and the abilities we can reach on our turn are 1-for-1s, not engines; holding 3 mana for it costs Kaito/Curiosity turns. Sideboard slots go to Day of Black Sun first (X=2 wipes tokens, Voice, Took, Headliner, Queen at sorcery speed, immune to Voice; also kills our own MV-2-or-less board, so time it), then Duress (sorcery speed, takes Song / Hop / Call / Triumph), then Faebloom Trick (two flying tokens for Curiosity attacks and chump blocks). Flashfreeze only if you already have a plan to keep Voice off the table: Song, Call and Hop are cast on their turn, so under Voice the only red spells it can hit are Torch and an instant-speed Frontline Rush on our turn. Annul is for Rest in Peace if they show it. Net: stock 1 Tidebinder + 2 Wasp main is right; post-board 2 Wasp, 1 Tidebinder, plus Day of Black Sun and Duress.
Review trail: confidence high, 7 disputed, 18 changes
Disputed reviewer points (kept as written)
  • Reviewer 3's correction that Tidebinder can counter Voice's Mobilize 2 'when a second Voice is attacking after the first was Wasped': rejected. The second Voice is un-blanked, so its static 'Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn' locks us out while it attacks. Reviewer 1's stronger version (the window never exists) is what the page now says.
  • Reviewer 2's correction 'Drowner ETB on Voice on our turn; shuffle at THEIR upkeep next turn': rejected on two counts. (1) Drowner's shuffle has {T} in its cost; a creature that entered during our turn N has not been under our control since the beginning of our most recent turn during their turn N, so it cannot activate then (CR 302.6). (2) Their turn-N untap step removes Voice's only stun counter instead of untapping it, so by our turn N+1 Voice has no stun counter and is not a legal target for the shuffle. My own draft's version of this line was also wrong for reason (2); replaced with the two sequences that do work (Drowner at their end step after Wasp blanks Voice, or Kaito's −2 supplying two stun counters).
  • Reviewer 2's list of our 'under-Voice actions' included Kaito's loyalty abilities and Spyglass Siren's Map: rejected for the their-turn claim. Loyalty abilities are activated only as a sorcery (CR 606.3) and the Map token says 'Activate only as a sorcery'. Restless Reef, Soulstone Sanctuary, Drowner's shuffle and Ghost Vacuum are the real under-Voice activations.
  • Reviewer 2's claim that Wasp on Voice into open Torch 'does not survive' the response sequence: accepted as strategy, and the rules basis is stated explicitly on the page: if Wasp leaves before Wasp's Sting resolves, the 'for as long as Wasp remains on the battlefield' duration has already ended, so the loses-all-abilities effect does nothing (the tap still resolves). This is the Wasp analogue of Tidebinder ruling 1.
  • Captain Marvel indestructible-counter footnote (Wasp entry only; removed from Tidebinder entry as unreachable, since power-up can be activated only once and Tidebinder can only blank her by countering that one activation). Kept as an explicitly hedged medium-confidence footnote: keyword counters carry the timestamp of their placement in layer 6, so a counter placed before Wasp's effect began loses its keyword while Wasp lives and a counter placed after is kept (ruling 2). Not derivable from the three supplied Tidebinder rulings alone; one sideboard copy, no load-bearing advice depends on it.
  • Delayed end-step sacrifice trigger on Mobilize Warriors: accepted reviewers 1 and 3 that its source is the Mobilize creature (CR 603.7d), so countering Headliner's version would blank Headliner. Kept 'do not target it' as a cost/benefit verdict (they keep the Warriors, and Voice's version is unreachable under the lockout), not a legality claim.
  • Reviewer 2's 'confidence: medium' request: after the revision no fatal or major finding survives, the Wasp verdict and hold/fire advice are rewritten around Requiting Hex, every rules claim on the page is grounded in the deck file's oracle text or the rules brief, and the single hedged edge case is labelled as such, so confidence is set to high.
Changes after review
  • Re-read the full Boros Tokens 75 and our Dimir 75 oracle text from /Users/emersonspartz/dimir-tidebinder-wasp-guide/data/decks/standard-boros-tokens-woe.json and standard-dimir-midrange-woe.json (Requiting Hex, Floodpits Drowner, Restless Reef, Day of Black Sun, We Say Thee Nay!, Kaito, Wan Shi Tong, Soulstone Sanctuary, Ghost Vacuum, Spyglass Siren) before revising.
  • Removed every claim that Tidebinder can counter Voice of Victory's Mobilize 2 (Kaito trap, Wasp target 1, Tidebinder trap 1); the window never exists because Voice attacking means an un-blanked Voice on its controller's turn.
  • Fixed Restless Reef line: the Shark has no flying or reach and cannot block an un-blanked Captain Marvel; deleted the garbled 'Frontline Rush pump target' phrase; reframed the line as the list of activated abilities that still work under Voice's spell-only lockout (Reef, Sanctuary, Drowner shuffle, Ghost Vacuum) and noted Kaito loyalty and Maps are sorcery-speed.
  • Rewrote the Floodpits Drowner 'permanent fix' line after finding my own error: a Drowner cast on our turn is summoning-sick through their turn and their untap step removes the single stun counter, so it cannot shuffle that Voice; replaced with the Wasp-then-Drowner-at-their-end-step sequence and the Kaito −2 (two stun counters) plus pre-existing Drowner sequence.
  • Downgraded Wasp from premium to good and rebuilt the verdict, hold/fire and counts around Requiting Hex as the default answer to Voice/Took/Headliner/Queen (all MV 2 or less); added the rules reason not to Wasp into open red (Wasp leaving before Wasp's Sting resolves means the blank never begins).
  • Added Stadium Headliner's Mobilize 1 as a real Tidebinder target (Headliner is on the battlefield when it resolves, so it blanks Headliner and denies the token and all engine triggers) and rewrote the contradictory dont_bother entry as a Took-vs-Headliner judgment call.
  • Added Dalkovan Encampment's 'Whenever you attack this turn' delayed trigger as the correct counter moment (after their mana is spent), land source so nothing blanks; moved the activation itself to dont_bother.
  • Reordered Tidebinder targets: Took, Headliner sac-ping (the window that recurs), Queen, Headliner Mobilize 1, Political Triumph (now 'only if it lines up, do not track counters'), Encampment trigger, Marvel (compressed to a footnote), Fountainport, Call.
  • Removed the impossible indestructible-counter footnote from the Tidebinder Marvel entry; kept it in the Wasp Marvel entry as an explicitly hedged medium-confidence note.
  • Corrected the delayed end-step sacrifice trap: its source is the Mobilize creature (CR 603.7d) so it would blank Headliner; verdict kept as a bad trade rather than an illegal or empty target; Encampment's version blanks nothing.
  • Fixed on-the-draw advice: Spell Snare ({U}) counters the turn-2 Voice spell with one untapped land; the lockout does not exist while Voice is on the stack.
  • Fixed Teamwork instruction: tap Wasp alone (power 2) for We Say Thee Nay!'s {4} tax.
  • Fixed Rest in Peace reasoning: its replacement effect exiles Enduring Curiosity instead of letting it die and return; still not a Tidebinder target; answer is Annul or removal.
  • Added Day of Black Sun symmetry: our Wasp, Drowner, Beavers, Siren and Wan Shi Tong (MV 2 on the battlefield) die at X=2; Tidebinder, Curiosity and Kaito survive.
  • Added the Kaito −2 upside to its trap entry (Voice cannot attack through two untap steps, enabling Drowner's shuffle) while keeping the rules point that stun does not lift the static.
  • Added Fountainport Treasure as a Took-only trigger window on our turn (Treasure is a token but not a creature).
  • Deprioritised Flashfreeze in the sideboard plan (under Voice it only ever hits Torch or an instant-speed Rush on our turn); Day of Black Sun and Duress first.
  • Removed 'face-up' wording and the garbled 'Voice eats a Torch' sentence; removed em dashes from all prose.
vs. Boros Dragons1.6% TB: situationalWasp: good▼
Curve of hasty fliers: Momo T1, Voice of Victory or Sarkhan T2, a warped Nova Hellkite T2-T3 (Momo makes the {2}{R} warp cost {1}{R}), Smaug / Magmatic Hellkite / Clarion Conqueror T3-T4, Lorehold or Dragonhawk T5, with Sarkhan and Smaug Treasures and Maelstrom of the Spirit Dragon tutoring the next Dragon. The engine against us is two STATIC abilities: Voice of Victory ("Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn") locks out every flash creature and counterspell we own on their turn, and Clarion Conqueror ("Activated abilities of artifacts, creatures, and planeswalkers can't be activated") switches off Kaito's loyalty abilities, Floodpits Drowner's shuffle ability and our Map tokens. Cavern of Souls (naming Dragon) makes the Dragons themselves uncounterable. Burst Lightning and Erode kill either of our lock pieces; Nova Hellkite's 1-damage ping and Charring Bite (5 damage to a creature without flying) each kill only one of them (the ping kills the 2/1 Wasp, Charring Bite kills the 3/2 Tidebinder). Neither Tidebinder nor a counterspell can touch a static, so the matchup is: kill Voice on OUR turn (Requiting Hex is the main tool, 4 copies, MV 2 target), blank Clarion with Wasp, then play the instant-speed game we are built for.
Tishana's Tidebinder
Tidebinder has real targets here (Lorehold's loot, Smaug's triggers, Dragonhawk, Magmatic Hellkite's land destruction) but the two cards that actually beat us, Voice of Victory and Clarion Conqueror, are statics it cannot touch, and Voice also stops Tidebinder from being cast during their turn at all, which is when almost every Dragon trigger happens. While a Voice with abilities is on the battlefield the only live windows are on OUR turn: Lorehold's loot trigger in our upkeep, a Maelstrom activation at our end step, or a plain 3/2 flash body (weak here: their threats fly, a 3/2 blocks only Voice, Sarkhan and Warrior tokens). Once Voice is dead or blanked, Tidebinder becomes good and is our only 'counter' that works through Cavern of Souls, because Cavern protects the spell, not its ETB trigger. Early turns: develop (Beavers/Siren into Kaito ninjutsu, Curiosity on 4) rather than holding 3 mana for a speculative Hellkite ETB.
#1Lorehold, the Historian
"At the beginning of each opponent's upkeep, you may discard a card. If you do, draw a card." (the miracle-granting line is a static and is NOT the target)
WhyThe one high-value trigger in the whole deck that happens on OUR turn, so it works straight through Voice of Victory and we always have untapped mana for it. Countering it stops the loot, stops any miracle {2} cast the drawn card would enable (Split Up on our untapped team is the real threat, then Desolation of Smaug), and blanks a 5/5 flying haste into a 5/5 ground body for as long as Tidebinder lives. Wasp on Lorehold does NOT do the same job: Wasp cast in our main phase only stops the NEXT upkeep's loot, and only if a 2/1 survives their turn.
WhenOur upkeep, immediately after the trigger goes on the stack (we are the active player and get priority first). Every Tidebinder copy is live here regardless of Voice.
AfterLoses: flying, haste, "Each instant and sorcery card in your hand has miracle {2}", the upkeep loot. Keeps: 5/5 body. Cards in their hand stop having miracle while Tidebinder lives.
RiskErode, Burst Lightning or Charring Bite on Tidebinder during their turn returns Lorehold to full; the loot that turn is still gone.
#2Smaug the Magnificent
"Whenever Smaug attacks, he deals damage equal to the number of Treasures you control to any target." (alternative window: "At the beginning of your upkeep, create a Treasure token.")
WhyEither trigger blanks a hasty 4/3 flier for as long as Tidebinder lives. The attack-trigger window also stops the damage (often only 1-2, since this deck spends Treasures on Dragons, but it can be aimed at Kaito's loyalty) and leaves a wingless 4/3 already committed to attacking: Enduring Curiosity (4/3) blocks and kills it (Curiosity dies too and returns as an enchantment unless Rest in Peace is out), or Tidebinder (3/2) blocks and trades. Kaito never blocks: he is a creature only during OUR turn.
WhenTheir declare attackers step, attack trigger on the stack, target chosen. Only possible if no Voice of Victory with abilities is on the battlefield. Earlier alternative: their upkeep with the Treasure trigger on the stack, which blanks Smaug before it attacks at all. On the play we have 3 lands on their turn 3; on the draw we have 2, so Smaug's first attack is usually uncontestable on the draw.
AfterLoses: flying, haste, the attack-damage trigger, the upkeep Treasure trigger. Keeps: 4/3 body, legendary status, Treasures already made (they still tap for mana). Effect ends the moment Tidebinder leaves the battlefield.
RiskBurst Lightning (2 damage) or Charring Bite kills Tidebinder at instant/sorcery speed and Smaug gets everything back.
#3Dragonhawk, Fate's Tempest
"Whenever Dragonhawk enters or attacks, exile the top X cards of your library, where X is the number of creatures you control with power 4 or greater. You may play those cards until your next end step. At the beginning of your next end step, Dragonhawk deals 2 damage to each opponent for each of those cards that are still exiled."
WhyOne trigger is X free cards plus 2 damage per unplayed card (X counts Dragonhawk itself, so at least 1, typically 3-4). Countering the enters/attacks trigger means nothing is exiled and the end-step damage is never created; Dragonhawk is a 5/5 ground body while Tidebinder lives.
WhenTheir main phase with the ETB on the stack, or their declare attackers step with the attack trigger on the stack. Voice-gated. Backup window: the delayed trigger at the beginning of THEIR next end step is itself a triggered ability and can be countered (Voice-gated). Its source is Dragonhawk (CR 603.7e: a delayed trigger created by a triggered ability has that ability's source): if Dragonhawk is still on the battlefield, countering the delayed damage also blanks Dragonhawk; if Dragonhawk already died (Bitter Triumph), the damage still fires by last-known information and Tidebinder on it is the only way to stop it, though then nothing loses abilities.
AfterLoses: flying and the enters/attacks trigger. Keeps: 5/5 body. Cards already exiled by a previously resolved trigger stay playable and an already-created end-step damage trigger still fires unless countered separately.
RiskSingle copy in the main deck; do not hold Tidebinder waiting for it.
#4Magmatic Hellkite
"When this creature enters, destroy target nonbasic land an opponent controls. Its controller searches their library for a basic land card, puts it onto the battlefield tapped with a stun counter on it, then shuffles."
Why15 of our 23 lands are nonbasic. The replacement basic enters tapped with a stun counter, so our next untap step removes the counter instead of untapping it and it untaps the turn after: one lost untap step plus the utility land itself (Restless Reef, Soulstone Sanctuary, Hidden Lair). Countering keeps the land and turns a 4/5 flier into a 4/5 ground body our team can block. (We search our own library, so Wan Shi Tong does not trigger.)
WhenTheir main phase, ETB on the stack. Voice-gated. Realistic only once Voice is handled; do not skip Kaito on turn 3 to hold for it.
AfterLoses: flying and the ETB. Keeps: 4/5 body.
RiskIf they cast it with Cavern mana our counterspells were never an option anyway, so this is the correct answer, not a fallback.
#5Maelstrom of the Spirit Dragon
"{4}, {T}, Sacrifice this land: Search your library for a Dragon card, reveal it, put it into your hand, then shuffle."
WhyA non-mana activated ability with no target, so it is legal. Costs stay paid: the land is already sacrificed, the 4 mana is spent and any Treasures cracked for it are gone, and they get no Dragon. Because they usually crack it at OUR end step it is castable through Voice. EXCEPTION: with Wan Shi Tong, Librarian on our battlefield, let it resolve: "Whenever an opponent searches their library, put a +1/+1 counter on Wan Shi Tong and draw a card" is a free card for us.
WhenAny time the ability is on the stack; most often our end step. If they crack it on their own turn under Voice we cannot respond.
AfterNothing loses abilities (the source is a land and it is already in the graveyard). Sacrificed Treasures do not come back.
RiskA careful pilot will not crack it into 3 open blue mana; the value is mostly in forcing them to sequence badly.
#6Sheltered by Ghosts (sideboard)
"When this Aura enters, exile target nonland permanent an opponent controls until this Aura leaves the battlefield."
WhyTheir post-board answer to Kaito, Enduring Curiosity or Wan Shi Tong. Countering the ETB saves our permanent.
WhenTheir main phase, Aura ETB on the stack. Voice-gated. The enchanted creature has ward {2} at that moment, but ward never taxes Tidebinder: ward triggers only when the warded permanent itself becomes a target, and Tidebinder targets the ability on the stack.
AfterNothing loses abilities (the source is an enchantment). The Aura stays and keeps granting +1/+0, lifelink and ward {2}.
RiskAnnul on the Aura spell is cleaner if we are allowed to cast it (no Voice with abilities out).
#7Seam Rip (sideboard)
"When this enchantment enters, exile target nonland permanent an opponent controls with mana value 2 or less until this enchantment leaves the battlefield."
WhyIts legal targets in our deck are Wasp, Drowner, Spyglass Siren, Dream Beavers and Map tokens; worth countering only when the target is a Wasp holding Voice or Clarion blank, or a Drowner locking a Dragon.
WhenTheir main phase, ETB on the stack. Voice-gated (unless Wasp has Voice blanked, which is exactly when this matters).
AfterNothing loses abilities (source is an enchantment). Seam Rip stays as a blank enchantment.
RiskSpending 3 mana to protect a 2-mana creature is only right when that creature is the lock piece.
#8Nova Hellkite (ETB ping)
"When this creature enters, it deals 1 damage to target creature an opponent controls."
WhyThe ping kills exactly our X/1s: Wasp (2/1), Floodpits Drowner (2/1), Spyglass Siren (1/1), Beavers (1/1); it never kills Tidebinder (3/2). Counter it only when the target is a Wasp holding Voice/Clarion blank or a Drowner holding a stunned Dragon. Nova also loses flying and haste for that combat.
WhenTheir main phase, ETB on the stack. Voice-gated, but if Wasp is blanking Voice we CAN respond (Voice has no static while Wasp lives; a spell already on the stack resolves even if Wasp then dies).
AfterLoses: flying, haste, the ETB. Keeps: 4/5 body. If Nova was WARPED it is still exiled at the beginning of the next end step: the warp exile is a delayed triggered ability created while the spell was on the stack (CR 702.185a), not an ability of the permanent, and CR 603.7c says a delayed trigger still affects the object even if its characteristics change. There is no 'permanent vanilla 4/5' upside.
RiskOnly take this trade to save a lock piece; against a warped Nova you are buying one grounded combat and the X/1's life, nothing more.
#9Monica Rambeau
Front: "{2}{R}{W}{W}: Transform Monica Rambeau. Activate only as a sorcery." Back: "Whenever you cast a noncreature spell, put a +1/+1 counter on each other creature you control" and prowess ("Whenever you cast a noncreature spell, this creature gets +1/+1 until end of turn").
WhyCountering the transform wastes their whole turn's mana and blanks her (loses flying, prowess, transform). The back face is hexproof, but Tidebinder targets the ABILITY, so either of her two back-face cast triggers can be countered and she then loses hexproof too while Tidebinder lives.
WhenTransform: their main phase, activation on the stack. Back-face triggers: whenever they cast a noncreature spell (Burst Lightning, Erode). All Voice-gated. Their own Clarion Conqueror stops them activating the transform at all.
AfterLoses: flying, prowess, transform (front) or flying, hexproof, prowess, counter trigger (back). Keeps: printed body, counters already on other creatures.
RiskSingle copy; opportunistic only.
#10Miracle cast trigger from Lorehold (any instant/sorcery in their hand)
Miracle {2} (CR 702.94a): "You may reveal this card from your hand as you draw it if it's the first card you've drawn this turn. When you reveal this card this way, you may cast it by paying {2} rather than its mana cost." The reveal is a static; the counterable piece is the "When you reveal this card this way" trigger.
WhyIf Lorehold's loot already resolved (we missed the window) and they reveal Split Up or Desolation of Smaug in our upkeep, countering the reveal trigger stops that cast. Strictly worse than countering the loot itself, which prevents the draw. Miracle Burst Lightning for {2} is worse than its normal {R}, so the real miracle threats are Split Up and Desolation.
WhenOur upkeep, after the loot resolves and they reveal the drawn card. Works through Voice (our turn).
AfterNothing loses abilities: the source is a card in their hand, not a permanent. The card stays in hand and can be cast normally later.
RiskWe could instead let the spell be cast and counter it with a real counterspell (our turn, so legal), which also handles it.
Traps
Voice of Victory: Tidebinder in response to Voice to shut off the lock. "Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn" is a static ability. Tidebinder counters only activated and triggered abilities. Worse, once Voice is on the battlefield you cannot cast Tidebinder during their turn at all. Handle Voice on your own turn with Requiting Hex (MV 2 target), Shoot the Sheriff, Bitter Triumph or Wasp, or Spell Snare the Voice spell (MV 2) while it is on the stack, which is legal only if no OTHER Voice with abilities is already on the battlefield.
Voice of Victory (Mobilize 2): Counter the mobilize trigger when Voice attacks. "Whenever this creature attacks, create two tapped and attacking 1/1 red Warrior creature tokens" is a real triggered ability, but Voice is on the battlefield when it attacks, so its static forbids us casting anything during their turn. The only Voice that can attack while we can respond is one already blanked by Wasp, and that one has no mobilize. In practice mobilize is never counterable.
Clarion Conqueror: Tidebinder Clarion to turn Kaito's loyalty abilities back on. "Activated abilities of artifacts, creatures, and planeswalkers can't be activated" is static; Clarion has no activated or triggered ability to target. Wasp (loses all abilities) or removal fixes it (Requiting Hex cannot: Clarion is MV 3). Clarion does not stop Tidebinder's or Wasp's ETBs (triggered) and does not stop Kaito's ninjutsu (an ability of a card in hand; Clarion's wording refers to permanents on the battlefield, CR 109.2).
Nova Hellkite (warp): Counter the warp cast or the recast from exile. Casting a spell for its warp cost is an alternative cost, and casting from exile is casting. Neither is an ability. Only the ETB ping and the end-step exile trigger are abilities.
Nova Hellkite (warp exile trigger): Counter "exile this creature at the beginning of the next end step" so the warped Nova stays. You would be converting their 2-mana rental into a Dragon they never have to pay 5 to recast. Even if Nova loses abilities while Tidebinder lives (whether it does is a judge question: the trigger was created by a static of the SPELL, CR 702.185a/603.7g, and we cannot confirm the permanent counts as the same source), the moment Tidebinder dies to Burst Lightning, Erode or Charring Bite they own a permanent 4/5 flying haste. Also Voice-gated like every other their-turn window. Never.
Nova Hellkite (blanked while warped): Blank a warped Nova with Tidebinder or Wasp and it stays as a vanilla 4/5. Wrong. The warp exile is a delayed triggered ability created while the spell was on the stack (CR 702.185a); losing all abilities on the battlefield does not touch it (CR 603.7c gives exactly this example). A blanked warped Nova is still exiled at the next end step and comes back later as a new object with all its abilities.
Momo, Friendly Flier: Counter the cost reduction so Nova cannot be warped for 2. "The first non-Lemur creature spell with flying you cast during each of your turns costs {1} less to cast" is static. Wasp can blank it, Requiting Hex kills it (MV 1); Tidebinder cannot touch it.
Lorehold, the Historian (miracle line): Counter "Each instant and sorcery card in your hand has miracle {2}". That line is static and grants an ability to cards in hand. The counterable thing is the loot trigger in our upkeep (priority 1) or the miracle reveal trigger of the drawn card, whose source is a card in hand, so Lorehold loses nothing when you counter that.
Treasure tokens, Maelstrom's mana, Cavern of Souls, Sunbillow Verge: Counter the mana to stop the Dragon. All mana abilities; Tidebinder cannot target them (reminder text on the card). Cavern's "that spell can't be countered" rides on a mana ability and protects the spell only; the Dragon's ETB trigger is fair game.
Burst Lightning, Erode, Charring Bite (Twinmaw omen), Desolation of Smaug, Split Up, Dawn's Truce: Tidebinder the burn or the sweeper. Spells, not abilities. Kicker on Burst Lightning is an additional cost paid while casting. Desolation's "Add four mana" is part of a spell's effect. Use Spell Snare (Charring Bite and Dawn's Truce are MV 2) or a hard counter, only during a turn no Voice with abilities is out.
Sheltered by Ghosts / Seam Rip / Rest in Peace: Counter the ETB and the enchantment is neutralized. The ETB is countered (good), but the source is an enchantment, so nothing loses abilities: Sheltered keeps granting +1/+0, lifelink and ward {2}, and Rest in Peace's replacement effect ("If a card or token would be put into a graveyard from anywhere, exile it instead") never uses the stack and can never be countered. That replacement stops Enduring Curiosity from ever dying and returning.
Multiversal Passage, Sacred Foundry, Cavern of Souls, Inspiring Vantage: Counter the 'as this enters' choice or the tapped clause. "As this land enters" clauses are replacement effects and "enters tapped unless" is static. Nothing goes on the stack.
Doorkeeper Thrull (sideboard): Tidebinder the Thrull, or cast Tidebinder into open W mana after they show Thrull. "Artifacts and creatures entering don't cause abilities to trigger" is static and also switches off Tidebinder's and Wasp's own ETBs. Thrull has flash: with 2 open mana they can respond to a Tidebinder cast and it enters with no trigger. Kill Thrull (Requiting Hex, MV 2; Shoot the Sheriff; Bitter Triumph) before deploying either card.
Voice of Victory tokens: Counter "Sacrifice them at the beginning of the next end step". It is a delayed trigger and technically counterable, but countering it just lets THEM keep two 1/1s, and Voice's static is on the battlefield at their end step anyway. Never.
Sarkhan, Dragon Ascendant (turn 2): 'Nothing answers the turn-2 Sarkhan.' Tidebinder cannot be cast on their turn 2 (we have at most 2 lands), but Spell Snare counters the MV-2 Sarkhan spell, and Requiting Hex kills it on our turn.
Not worth it
Sarkhan's two triggers (behold Treasure on ETB, +1/+1 counter and flying when a Dragon enters): after turn 3 the Treasure is minor and the counter is a 2/2 growing by one; if you Tidebinder Sarkhan you are really just casting a 3/2 body. Requiting Hex (MV 2) is the real Sarkhan answer.Momo's "Whenever another creature you control with flying enters, Momo gets +1/+1 until end of turn": one temporary point of power. Hex kills Momo for {B}.Twinmaw Stormbrood's "When this creature enters, you gain 5 life": 5 life is not worth 3 mana and a card; blanking a 5/4 flier is the only upside, and Wasp does that for 2.Monica's front-face prowess and back-face prowess: +1/+1 until end of turn; only relevant as the ability you target to strip hexproof (priority 9).Rest in Peace's ETB (exile all graveyards): our deck barely uses the graveyard and the replacement effect that actually hurts Curiosity cannot be countered.Smaug's upkeep Treasure trigger when Smaug will attack anyway: fine as a window (and the only Smaug window on their upkeep), but the attack trigger is the same blank with a better board result (a wingless attacker Curiosity can block and kill).Maelstrom's tutor when Wan Shi Tong is on our battlefield: letting them search draws us a card and grows Wan Shi Tong.The 3/2 body as a blocker: every real threat in this deck flies (Nova, Magmatic, Smaug, Lorehold, Dragonhawk, Clarion, Twinmaw, Momo, Thrull); Tidebinder blocks only Voice, Sarkhan and Warrior tokens. Fire it as a body late rather than never, but do not count it as value.
The Wondrous Wasp
Wasp is the better of our two cards in this matchup because it strips STATIC abilities, which is exactly what Tidebinder cannot do here. Its default job is Clarion Conqueror ("Activated abilities of artifacts, creatures, and planeswalkers can't be activated"): nothing else in our 75 except Shoot the Sheriff, Bitter Triumph and Day of Black Sun X=3 answers a MV-3 Clarion, and Wasp turns Kaito, Drowner and Maps back on the same turn. Voice of Victory is Wasp's second job, not its first: we run 4 Requiting Hex (destroy creature with MV 2 or less, Voice is {1}{W}), 2 Shoot the Sheriff and 2 Bitter Triumph, all castable on our turn under the lock, so fire Wasp at Voice only when none of those is in hand. Voice also stops us casting Wasp on their turn, so the Voice blank is a our-turn play. The body is a 2/1 flier that dies to Burst Lightning, Erode or Nova Hellkite's 1-damage ping (Charring Bite cannot hit it: flying), which ends the effect instantly. Use the window it buys rather than counting on it lasting.
#1Clarion Conqueror
WhyClarion turns Kaito into a hexproof 3/4 that cannot use loyalty abilities, stops Floodpits Drowner's shuffle ability and our Map tokens (and their own Treasures and Monica's transform). Requiting Hex cannot kill it (MV 3), so this is Wasp's job. Wasp blanks it and Kaito comes back online the same turn. Clarion also loses flying, so it is a 3/3 ground body instead of a flying blocker for Wasp and Siren.
WhenOur main phase 1 (before activating Kaito or Drowner). Also fine on their turn if no Voice with abilities is out. Tapping it before their attackers only matters if it is their only flier.
AfterLoses the activation lock and flying; keeps 3/3. Kaito's loyalty abilities, Drowner's ability and Maps work while Wasp lives.
#2Voice of Victory
Why"Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn" is the whole reason our deck stumbles here. Wasp removes it (and mobilize), so on their next turn Tidebinder, counterspells, Bitter Triumph and Spell Snare all work again. Voice becomes a 1/3 with nothing. Prefer Requiting Hex ({B}, instant, MV-2 target), Shoot the Sheriff or Bitter Triumph on Voice and keep Wasp for Clarion; use Wasp when no removal is in hand or the removal is needed for a Dragon.
WhenOUR turn only (their turn is locked while Voice has abilities). Main phase 1 if Kaito or Curiosity are attacking into Voice (the tap removes the blocker); otherwise at our end step so our 2 mana stay open through our own turn (miracle response in upkeep, Thrull check). They see the Wasp either way.
AfterVoice loses the spell lock and mobilize; keeps 1/3. A second Voice on the battlefield still locks us: each Voice needs its own answer. If Wasp dies, the lock returns immediately, but spells we already put on the stack still resolve.
#3Smaug the Magnificent
WhyBlanked Smaug has no flying, no haste, no Treasure and no attack damage: a 4/3 that Enduring Curiosity (4/3) blocks and kills (trade; Curiosity returns as an enchantment unless Rest in Peace is out) or Tidebinder (3/2) trades with. Kaito cannot block on their turn. Tapping it before attackers skips a whole attack.
WhenTheir beginning of combat step, BEFORE attackers are declared (requires no Voice, or a Voice already blanked). Tapping an already-attacking Smaug does not remove it from combat. On our turn: tap it so it cannot block and blank the upkeep Treasure it would make next turn.
AfterLoses flying, haste, both triggers; keeps 4/3 and its existing Treasures.
#4Lorehold, the Historian
WhyBlank removes flying, haste, the miracle grant and the loot, and the tap keeps a 5/5 from blocking Curiosity or Kaito. But for the loot itself Tidebinder in our upkeep is better: it stops THAT loot now, while Wasp only stops the next upkeep's loot and only if the 2/1 survives their turn (3 Burst Lightning, 1-2 Erode, 4 Nova pings).
WhenOur main phase to ground it for combat, or their beginning of combat if Voice is gone.
AfterLoses flying, haste, miracle grant, upkeep loot; keeps 5/5.
#5Dragonhawk, Fate's Tempest
WhyThe attack trigger is the repeatable value; a Wasp resolved before attackers means no attack trigger and no flying.
WhenTheir beginning of combat before attackers (Voice gone), or our turn. The ETB has usually already resolved; cards already exiled stay playable and the already-created end-step damage trigger still fires (Tidebinder can counter that one).
AfterLoses flying and the enters/attacks trigger; keeps 5/5.
#6Magmatic Hellkite / hard-cast Nova Hellkite (after their ETB)
WhyOnce the ETB has resolved they are 4/5 fliers. Wasp grounds one so our ground blockers can hold it and our fliers (Wasp, Siren, Beavers) get through for Enduring Curiosity draws; the tap skips one attack. A WARPED Nova is a poor Wasp target: it is exiled at the next end step regardless of the blank and returns later as a fresh object, so Wasp on it buys exactly one skipped attack (4 damage). Do it only when those 4 life decide the game (e.g. on the play vs Momo T1 into warped Nova T2, Wasp at their beginning of combat is our only answer) or when Wasp has no other target.
WhenTheir beginning of combat before attackers (Voice gone) to stop an attack; our turn to remove a flying blocker.
AfterLoses flying (and haste on Nova); keeps 4/5. A blanked warped Nova is still exiled at the beginning of the next end step (CR 702.185a, 603.7c).
Traps
Voice of Victory: Flash Wasp at their beginning of combat to tap Voice and stop mobilize. While Voice has abilities we cannot cast Wasp during their turn. The Voice blank is a our-turn play, full stop.
Voice of Victory (default Wasp target): Wasp is the Voice answer. Requiting Hex (4 copies, {B}, instant, destroy creature with MV 2 or less) kills Voice permanently on our turn and also hits Momo, Sarkhan, Doorkeeper Thrull and Warrior tokens; Shoot the Sheriff and Bitter Triumph also kill it. Wasp is the only cheap answer to Clarion Conqueror (MV 3), so spend Hex on Voice and Wasp on Clarion whenever both are in hand.
Voice of Victory (Kaito -2 / Drowner as the answer): Kaito -2 or Floodpits Drowner's stun handles Voice. Tap plus stun counters do nothing to a static ability; Voice keeps locking us while tapped. Only losing all abilities (Wasp, Day of Black Sun) or removal ends it.
Any attacking creature: Wasp an attacker to remove it from combat. Tapping an attacking creature does not remove it from combat; it still deals damage. Only blanking matters at that point (Smaug's already-triggered attack damage is not stopped by Wasp either; that is a Tidebinder job).
Nova Hellkite (warped): Wasp a freshly warped Nova to keep it on the battlefield as a vanilla 4/5. The warp exile is a delayed trigger created while the spell was on the stack (CR 702.185a); losing all abilities does not touch it (CR 603.7c). Nova leaves at end step and comes back later with everything. Wasp on it only skips one attack.
Monica Rambeau (transformed): Wasp the transformed Monica. Back face has hexproof; Wasp's trigger cannot target her. Tidebinder can still counter her cast triggers because it targets the ability, not the creature.
Creature wearing Sheltered by Ghosts: Wasp it for 2 mana. Ward {2}: pay 2 more when Wasp's trigger targets it or the trigger is countered. Ward never taxes Tidebinder: ward triggers only when the warded permanent itself becomes a target, and Tidebinder targets the ability on the stack, not the creature.
Dawn's Truce (sideboard): Wasp during a turn they cast Dawn's Truce. "You and permanents you control gain hexproof until end of turn": Wasp's trigger has no legal target. Tidebinder is unaffected (it targets abilities).
Doorkeeper Thrull (sideboard): Wasp with Thrull on the battlefield or into 2 open W mana. "Artifacts and creatures entering don't cause abilities to trigger": Wasp enters, nothing happens, and it cannot even blank the Thrull. Remove Thrull first (Requiting Hex kills it: MV 2).
Second Voice of Victory: One Wasp fixes the lock. Each Voice's static is independent. With two Voices out, blanking one changes nothing until the other is also answered.
Momo / Sarkhan: Wasp Momo or Sarkhan as an 'ability' target. Momo's cost reducer and Sarkhan's Treasure/counter triggers are low value after turn 3; Wasp on them is just a 2/1 flier. Requiting Hex kills both for {B}. Save the blank for Clarion, Voice or a Dragon.
Lines with the rest of the deck
  • HEX LINE (the main unlock): Requiting Hex on Voice of Victory during our turn (instant, legal under the lock; Voice is MV 2). Their next turn every flash creature and counterspell is live again. Hex also answers Momo (MV 1), Sarkhan (MV 2), Doorkeeper Thrull (MV 2) and Warrior tokens; it can never hit Clarion (MV 3) or a Dragon.
  • WASP LINE (when no Hex/Shoot/Triumph in hand): our main phase, Wasp targets Voice (tap + blank). Their turn: Tidebinder is live for Smaug's attack trigger, Magmatic's ETB or Dragonhawk; Bitter Triumph, Spell Snare and hard counters work again. If they Burst Lightning the Wasp, respond while Wasp is still alive: Voice has no static until Wasp actually dies, and anything already on the stack resolves.
  • KAITO RESCUE: Wasp on Clarion Conqueror in main phase 1, then Kaito -2 on Smaug or a Hellkite (tap + two stun counters) the same turn, or Drowner's {1}{U},{T} shuffle on a stunned Dragon. Clarion never stopped Kaito's ninjutsu (card in hand) or our flash ETBs (triggered), only the activations.
  • SPELL SNARE: answers Voice (MV 2), Sarkhan (MV 2), Charring Bite (MV 2), Doorkeeper Thrull and Sheltered by Ghosts and Dawn's Truce, but only during a turn no Voice with abilities is on the battlefield. The Voice being cast is only a spell and does not lock us on its own, so Spell Snare on the FIRST Voice is always legal; on a second Voice it is legal only if the first is dead or blanked.
  • UPKEEP TIDEBINDER: with Lorehold on their side, keep 3 mana in mind for our own upkeep: Tidebinder on "At the beginning of each opponent's upkeep, you may discard a card. If you do, draw a card" works through Voice, kills the miracle-Split-Up risk, and leaves a 3/2 that blocks a 1/3 Voice and kills it.
  • SMAUG BLOCK: Tidebinder on Smaug's attack trigger, then block the now-wingless 4/3 with Tidebinder (both die, fine) or with Enduring Curiosity (kills Smaug; Curiosity dies and returns as an enchantment, unless Rest in Peace is out). Kaito is not a creature on their turn and cannot block.
  • CURIOSITY DRAWS: every blank removes flying, so Wasp (2/1 flying), Spyglass Siren and Dream Beavers connect in the air past grounded Dragons; each hit is a card with Enduring Curiosity on the battlefield.
  • CAVERN GAP: a Dragon cast with Cavern of Souls mana cannot be countered, but its ETB can. When they lead with Cavern, hold Tidebinder rather than a counterspell for the Hellkite, once Voice is handled.
  • DRAGONHAWK CLEANUP: Tidebinder on the delayed end-step damage trigger (Voice gone) stops the damage; if Dragonhawk is still alive it also blanks Dragonhawk (the delayed trigger's source is Dragonhawk, CR 603.7e); if Dragonhawk already died the damage is stopped and nothing loses abilities.
  • WAN SHI TONG + MAELSTROM: with Wan Shi Tong on our battlefield, let Maelstrom of the Spirit Dragon's tutor resolve: their search gives us a card and a +1/+1 counter. Without Wan Shi Tong, Tidebinder the activation at our end step (land and Treasures already spent, no Dragon).
  • EARLY TURNS: turns 1-4 develop (Beavers/Siren into Kaito ninjutsu on 3, Curiosity or Wan Shi Tong on 4). Do not skip Kaito to hold 3 mana for a speculative Hellkite ETB; with 4 Voice in their deck the hold is dead too often and the T3 Hellkite is usually Cavern-protected anyway. Tidebinder's early windows are 'nothing better to do with 3 mana'.
  • MOMO INTO WARPED NOVA (their T1-T2): not counterable (warp is a cast, Tidebinder needs 3 lands). On the play, Wasp at their turn-2 beginning of combat taps and grounds Nova before it attacks (if we have no creature, Nova's ping has no target and does nothing); Nova is still exiled at end step, so this buys 4 life and costs our turn-2 development. On the draw (1 land) there is no answer: block or race.
  • CHARRING BITE RISK: Twinmaw's omen ("5 damage to target creature without flying", sorcery) kills Tidebinder, Curiosity, Drowner, Wan Shi Tong and Beavers-less ground bodies but can never hit Wasp, Siren or Beavers (flying) or Kaito (not a creature on their turn). A Wasp lock is sturdier against it than a Tidebinder lock; Spell Snare counters it (MV 2) on a Voice-free turn.
  • DO NOT DEPLOY INTO THRULL (post-board): if they show 2 open mana with W and Doorkeeper Thrull is plausible, cast Tidebinder or Wasp only when the trigger does not matter, or bait Thrull with the less important one first; Hex the Thrull once it lands.
Hold or fire
While a Voice of Victory with abilities is on the battlefield: kill it on our turn with Requiting Hex first, Shoot the Sheriff or Bitter Triumph second, Wasp only if none of those is in hand; Tidebinder fires only at Lorehold's loot in our upkeep, at a Maelstrom activation on our turn (unless Wan Shi Tong is out), or as a 3/2 body at our end step when we need a blocker for Voice or tokens. Once Voice is gone or blanked: hold Wasp for Clarion Conqueror whenever Kaito is in hand or on the battlefield (Hex cannot touch Clarion), otherwise for Smaug or Dragonhawk at their beginning of combat before attackers; hold Tidebinder for Lorehold's loot (our upkeep), then Smaug's attack trigger (or upkeep Treasure), then Dragonhawk's enters/attacks trigger, then Magmatic's land destruction if it targets Restless Reef, Soulstone Sanctuary or Hidden Lair, and fire at Nova's ping or Seam Rip's ETB only when the target is a lock piece (Wasp on Voice/Clarion, Drowner on a stunned Dragon). Never counter Nova's warp exile trigger and never Wasp a warped Nova except to skip one game-deciding attack. Turns 1-4: develop, do not hold; their threats fly and our 3/2 body blocks nothing important, so Tidebinder as a body is a late-game consolation, not a plan. On the play we have 3 lands on their turn 3, so with no Voice out Tidebinder CAN answer a turn-3 Hellkite ETB, but only if we have nothing better (no Kaito ninjutsu) to do with the mana; on the draw we have 2 lands then, so Wasp is our only turn-3 flash play and Tidebinder starts at their turn 4.
How many to run
Keep stock: 1 Tidebinder main, 2 Wasp main (the stock list's choice; legendary limits copies on the battlefield, not in the deck), and do NOT bring the 2 sideboard Tidebinders in for this matchup. Reason: their two matchup-defining cards (Voice of Victory, Clarion Conqueror) are statics that Tidebinder cannot touch, and Voice removes almost every window Tidebinder wants (their-turn triggers). The one copy earns its slot for Lorehold's upkeep loot, Smaug's triggers and the Cavern gap. Both Wasps stay in: Wasp is the only cheap answer to Clarion. Our actual sideboard (Duress 3, Day of Black Sun 2, Annul 1, Ghost Vacuum 1, Spell Pierce 2, Strategic Betrayal 1, Vren 1, Flashfreeze 2, Faebloom Trick 2) contains no extra Shoot the Sheriff or Bitter Triumph, so the post-board plan is: Day of Black Sun (X=2 on our turn, legal under Voice: "Each creature with mana value X or less loses all abilities until end of turn. Destroy those creatures" kills Voice, Momo, Sarkhan, Thrull and tokens; X=3 adds Clarion and front-face Monica; it is symmetric and also kills our Beavers, Siren, Drowner, Wasp and Wan Shi Tong (MV 2 on the battlefield), Tidebinder at X=3, never Kaito at X<=3, so cast it into a near-empty board of ours), Annul for Sheltered by Ghosts / Seam Rip / Rest in Peace on a Voice-free turn, Duress for Split Up / Desolation / Burst Lightning. Skip Flashfreeze: it counters only red or green spells, so Voice, Clarion, Momo, Twinmaw, Thrull, Monica and every white sideboard card are immune, it only hits the Dragons and burn, and it is Voice-gated. Spell Pierce is Voice-gated too and hits little (Split Up, Desolation, Burst Lightning, the enchantments). Requiting Hex (4 main) is already the best Voice answer we own; never side it out here. If their list shows the 2 Doorkeeper Thrull, value every flash ETB a notch lower and lean on Hex and Day of Black Sun further.
Review trail: confidence high, 2 disputed, 22 changes
Disputed reviewer points (kept as written)
  • Reviewers 2 and 3: 'counter the warp exile trigger and Nova loses all abilities because its source is Nova (CR 603.7e / 603.7g)'. Partially disputed. CR 603.7e covers delayed triggers created by an activated or triggered ability; warp's exile is created by a static ability of the SPELL (CR 702.185a, verified in the current CR text), so 603.7g applies and the source is 'the object with that static ability', i.e. the spell, and whether the permanent it became counts as that same object for Tidebinder's artifact/creature/planeswalker check is not settled by any text we have. The final entry states this as a judge question and builds no line on it; the strategic conclusion (never counter that trigger) holds either way.
  • Reviewer 1 finding 5 ('Wasp at their turn-2 beginning of combat taps and blanks the warped Nova') vs reviewer 2 missed-target ('never Wasp a warped Nova'). Both are half right and neither is recorded as rejected: the Wasp play is legal and stops the hasty attack, but because the warp exile is a delayed trigger (CR 702.185a, 603.7c) the blank buys nothing beyond that one attack. The final entry says exactly that and makes it a conditional line, not a default.
Changes after review
  • FIXED (fatal): removed every claim that a blanked warped Nova Hellkite stays on the battlefield. Verified CR 702.185a (warp = two static abilities on the stack, one creating a delayed triggered ability) and CR 603.7c (a delayed trigger 'Exile this creature at the beginning of the next end step' still exiles the permanent after it changes characteristics). Rewrote Tidebinder target 8, Wasp target 6, both trap lists, and deleted the old medium-confidence hedge.
  • FIXED (major): Kaito removed from every blocking line; oracle text 'During your turn ... he's a 3/4 Ninja creature' means he is not a creature on their turn. Blocks on a grounded Smaug now list only Enduring Curiosity (trade, returns as enchantment) and Tidebinder (trade).
  • FIXED (major): 'Spell Snare on the Voice spell is always legal' replaced with 'legal only if no other Voice with abilities is already on the battlefield', in the trap, the combined line and hold_or_fire.
  • FIXED (major): removed the false 'Wasp is capped at 2 (legendary)' rule claim; stock runs 2 by choice.
  • FIXED (major): Maelstrom after_effect no longer says they keep Treasures; Treasure is sacrificed as a cost ('{T}, Sacrifice this artifact') and costs stay paid.
  • FIXED (major): Momo T1 into warped Nova T2 is now 'not counterable, but Wasp at their beginning of combat on the play taps and grounds it; no answer on the draw', with the honest note that Nova is still exiled at end step.
  • FIXED (major): added Requiting Hex (4 main, {B} instant, destroy creature MV 2 or less, verified from the mirror deck file) as the primary Voice answer; it also hits Momo, Sarkhan, Doorkeeper Thrull and tokens, never Clarion or Dragons. Wasp's default target is now Clarion Conqueror, Voice is second. hold_or_fire and counts rewritten around this.
  • FIXED (major): counts recommendation now names our real sideboard (no extra Shoot/Triumph exist): Day of Black Sun X=2/X=3 with its symmetric casualties (including Wan Shi Tong at MV 2), Annul, Duress; Flashfreeze flagged as red/green only and therefore useless against Voice/Clarion/Momo/Twinmaw/Thrull and all white sideboard cards.
  • FIXED (major): Tidebinder priorities reordered: Lorehold's upkeep loot (works through Voice, always castable) is #1, Smaug #2 with the damage reframed as a bonus (Treasures usually spent) and the blank+block as the real value.
  • FIXED (major): added the early-turn rule: develop into Kaito ninjutsu, do not hold 3 mana for a speculative Hellkite ETB.
  • FIXED (minor): stun counter math corrected to one lost untap step (Magmatic Hellkite).
  • FIXED (minor): Nova's 1-damage ping now stated to kill only X/1s (Wasp, Drowner, Siren, Beavers), never the 3/2 Tidebinder; Charring Bite added as the sorcery that kills Tidebinder but not Wasp.
  • FIXED (minor): miracle quoted from CR 702.94a (static linked to a triggered ability; the counterable piece is the 'When you reveal this card this way' trigger); noted miracle Burst Lightning for {2} is worse than {R}.
  • FIXED (minor): ward sentence rewritten: ward triggers only when the permanent itself becomes a target, so Tidebinder is never taxed.
  • FIXED (minor): Dragonhawk delayed trigger now covers both cases: alive (Dragonhawk is the source per CR 603.7e and loses all abilities) and dead (damage stopped, nothing blanked).
  • FIXED (minor): Wasp on Lorehold no longer described as 'the same job' as Tidebinder on the loot; Tidebinder stops the current loot, Wasp only the next one and only if it survives.
  • FIXED (minor): Nova warp exile trigger moved from dont_bother to traps (never counter it: converts a 2-mana rental into a permanent Dragon once Tidebinder dies).
  • FIXED (minor): collapsed Sarkhan/Momo/Monica-body targets into dont_bother with the plain statement that their threats fly and our bodies do not block them; Monica kept as a target only for the hexproof-stripping cast triggers (both back-face triggers now listed).
  • FIXED (minor): 'our end step to hide it' replaced with the real reason (keep 2 mana open through our own turn); Sarkhan 'uncounterable' corrected to 'Tidebinder cannot be cast on their turn 2; Spell Snare and Hex answer it'.
  • ADDED (missed target): Wan Shi Tong, Librarian ('Whenever an opponent searches their library, put a +1/+1 counter on Wan Shi Tong and draw a card') as a reason to let Maelstrom's tutor resolve; Magmatic and Erode make US search, so no trigger.
  • ADDED (missed target): Charring Bite as a Tidebinder-lock risk and a reason the Wasp lock is sturdier; Spell Snare answers it on a Voice-free turn.
  • Verification performed: re-read the Boros Dragons file and our stock list, pulled oracle text for Requiting Hex, Wan Shi Tong, Day of Black Sun, Flashfreeze, Kaito, Drowner, Curiosity and the rest of our 75 from the sibling deck files, fetched Scryfall rulings for warp (All-Fates Stalker) via the raw API, and grepped the current Comprehensive Rules for 702.185a, 702.109a, 702.152a, 702.94a, 702.21a, 702.181a, 603.7c/e/f/g.
vs. Azorius Tempo1.5% TB: situationalWasp: good▼
A flash-tempo deck that plays mostly on OUR turn. Turn 2 it drops a lock piece: Voice of Victory or Jennifer Walters ("Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn") or High Noon (4 copies, "Each player can't cast more than one spell each turn"); turn 3 Enduring Innocence (draw engine). Then it holds mana and flashes in answers at our end step or in response to our spells: Aven Interrupter (3-drop flyer, "exile target spell. It becomes plotted"), Aang, Swift Savior (3-drop flyer, "airbend up to one other target creature or spell"), Floodpits Drowner (2-mana ground 2/1, stun), Skycoach Conductor (2/3 flyer whose prepared copy of All Aboard blinks a creature, resetting anything we blanked or stunned), plus Bilbo's Gambit and Spell Pierce. It closes with 2-power evasive bodies, Enduring Curiosity draws, and 4/4 Bird tokens from The Eagles Are Coming!, with Settle the Wreckage and Avatar's Wrath as resets. Main has 21 creatures, 16 with flash, and only 10 of those carry an ETB trigger Tidebinder can counter (4 Interrupter, 3 Aang, 2 Drowner, 1 Wasp); the 4 Conductors have no triggered or activated ability at all.
Tishana's Tidebinder
Tidebinder has real windows here but they are gated. While Voice of Victory or Jennifer Walters is on the battlefield we cannot cast it on their turn at all; while High Noon is out, casting anything in our main phase means Tidebinder cannot be the response to whatever they flash in later that turn. Its best realistic jobs are on OUR turn: countering Aang's airbend aimed at a resolved Wan Shi Tong or Curiosity (or at the Wasp holding a blank), countering Interrupter/Aang aimed at our spell when we have 3 spare mana, blanking a Floodpits Drowner by countering its ETB, and stopping The Eagles Are Coming! tokens in our upkeep. Only 10 of their 21 creatures have an ETB it can hit; the 4 Skycoach Conductors and 2 Enduring Curiosity offer no ETB target. When no window exists, a 3/2 flash body at their end step (lock off) is fine, but it is a ground body against a deck of flyers. Their answers to the Tidebinder spell: Bilbo's Gambit, Aven Interrupter, Aang (Spell Pierce, Disdainful Stroke, Flashfreeze cannot touch a 3-mana blue creature). Their answers to a Tidebinder blank: Aang airbending Tidebinder (3), Skycoach Conductor blinking the blanked creature (4), Eagles bouncing it (2), Erode (1 main + 2 side), Pyrrhic Strike (side, MV 3 or greater), Avatar's Wrath (2), Settle the Wreckage if Tidebinder attacks. Do not count on any blank lasting more than one turn cycle.
#1Aang, Swift Savior
"When Aang enters, airbend up to one other target creature or spell. (Exile it. While it's exiled, its owner may cast it for {2} rather than its mana cost.)"
WhyThree copies and the most punishing thing they do to us. Airbend on a RESOLVED Wan Shi Tong wipes every +1/+1 counter and the {2} recast forces X=0 (Scryfall ruling 2025-10-02: 'If the spell you cast has {X} in its mana cost, you must choose 0'), so it comes back a 1/1 that draws nothing. Airbend on our Wasp/Tidebinder ends the blank it was holding. Airbend on our Kaito or Curiosity spell is a tempo hit (we do get a {2} recast, +{2} per Interrupter out). Countering the trigger saves the target and leaves Aang a 2/3 ground body with no flash, flying, airbend or Waterbend {8}.
WhenAang's ETB goes on the stack; Tidebinder in response; Tidebinder's trigger resolves first. They usually flash Aang at our end step or in response to our spell, so it is our turn and the Voice/Walters lock does not stop us, but High Noon does if we already cast a spell that turn. On their turn (Aang cast main phase at a resolved permanent) we can respond only if no lock piece is live. Note Kaito on the battlefield is never a legal airbend target (hexproof creature on our turn, planeswalker on theirs); only the Kaito spell is.
AfterAang stays a 2/3 with no abilities while Tidebinder lives. Counters stay. Anything Aang airbended earlier stays exiled. Ends the moment Tidebinder leaves.
RiskA second Aang or an Interrupter flashed in while Tidebinder's trigger is on the stack can still target our original spell (it is still on the stack) or a second permanent; Tidebinder counters one trigger. Conductor's blink on Aang gives a fresh airbend and ends the blank. Avatar's Wrath airbends Tidebinder along with everything.
#2Aven Interrupter
"When this creature enters, exile target spell. It becomes plotted."
WhyFour copies, their main answer to the Kaito / Enduring Curiosity / Wan Shi Tong spell. Countering the trigger means our spell resolves normally and Interrupter is a 2/2 with no flying and no "Spells your opponents cast from graveyards or from exile cost {2} more to cast". Same 3 mana they spent, and our 3/2 body outclasses their 2/2.
WhenOur turn, our main phase: they flash Interrupter in response to our spell, its ETB targets our spell, we flash Tidebinder targeting that trigger. Needs 3 SPARE mana beyond the spell we cast, and the spells they Interrupt cost 4+, so this is a 7-mana line: a late-game bonus, not the default plan. Dead under High Noon (our cast spell was our one spell). Works while Voice/Walters is out because it is our turn.
AfterInterrupter stays on the battlefield as a 2/2 with no abilities while Tidebinder lives: no flying, no {2} tax on our exile/graveyard casts. Flying and the tax return if Tidebinder dies, is blinked, bounced or airbended.
RiskA second Interrupter or an Aang flashed in while Tidebinder's trigger is on the stack has a legal target: our original spell, still on the stack beneath. Tidebinder counters only one trigger, so against two flash answers the line fails. They can also Gambit/Interrupt/airbend the Tidebinder spell itself. Conductor blink on Interrupter gives a fresh ETB against a later spell.
#3Floodpits Drowner (theirs)
ETB: "When this creature enters, tap target creature an opponent controls and put a stun counter on it." Activation: "{1}{U}, {T}: Shuffle this creature and target creature with a stun counter on it into their owners' libraries."
WhyThe shuffle is a permanent answer to our Curiosity/Beavers/Wan Shi Tong or to the Wasp holding a lock piece down. Countering the ETB is the best version: it saves the stun AND blanks Drowner (a creature on the battlefield as the trigger resolves), so the shuffle ability is gone for as long as Tidebinder lives. Countering the activation also works: Drowner stays tapped (cost paid), our creature stays (still stunned), Drowner is blanked.
WhenETB: on our turn when they flash Drowner in (lock does not matter; High Noon does if we cast a spell). Activation: whenever they have {1}{U} and an untapped Drowner facing a stunned creature; a good pilot fires it on their own turn under the lock, so our real window for the activation is when no lock piece is out. Kaito is never a legal Drowner target.
AfterDrowner remains a 2/1 (tapped if it activated) with no flash, vigilance, ETB or shuffle while Tidebinder lives. A stun counter already placed stays and expires normally. Note Conductor blinking our stunned creature removes the stun counter (new object, counters cease to exist) and ends any blank on it.
RiskThey run 2 Drowners; the stun counter remains a target for the second copy. Blanked Drowner comes back if Tidebinder leaves.
