#4The Eagles Are Coming!
Delayed trigger: "At the beginning of the next upkeep, create a 4/4 white Bird Soldier creature token with flying for each creature returned to your hand this way."
WhyOne triggered ability makes all the tokens, so a single Tidebinder stops every 4/4 (huge when kicked for 2-3 creatures). Unkicked it is still a 3/2 for their 4/4 flyer.
WhenThe next upkeep after Eagles resolves. If they cast Eagles on THEIR turn (e.g. in response to a Bitter Triumph we cast at their end step, which requires no lock piece live), the next upkeep is OURS and we respond freely; under High Noon this counter is our whole turn's spell, so weigh it against casting Kaito. If they cast Eagles on our turn (saving a creature from our removal), the trigger lands in THEIR upkeep, where a live Voice/Walters makes it uncounterable for us. Source is a spell card in the graveyard, so nothing loses abilities.
AfterNo tokens are created; the creatures they bounced are already in hand and stay there. Tidebinder is a 3/2 body; nothing is blanked.
RiskLow. They can Gambit/Interrupt the Tidebinder spell, but in upkeep they have little else to do.
#5Enduring Innocence
"Whenever one or more other creatures you control with power 2 or less enter, draw a card. This ability triggers only once each turn."
WhyEvery creature in their main deck except Enduring Curiosity (4/3) has power 2 or less, so Innocence draws off nearly every flash creature, and because they flash in on OUR turn the trigger is often on our turn where the lock does not apply. Countering it blanks Innocence: no lifelink, no draws, and no "When Enduring Innocence dies, if it was a creature, return it to the battlefield" while Tidebinder lives, so Bitter Triumph / Shoot the Sheriff then kills it for good.
WhenWhen the draw trigger goes on the stack, typically alongside an Interrupter/Conductor/Drowner ETB on our turn. If an Interrupter/Aang ETB aimed at our spell is on the stack at the same time, counter that instead unless the spell is cheap. Not reachable on their turn while a lock piece is live.
AfterInnocence remains a 2/1 with no abilities. Cards already drawn stay drawn. If it dies while blanked it does NOT return (it had no dies trigger to look back at). Effect ends if Tidebinder leaves.
RiskThe trigger already fired this turn; the blank matters more than the counter. Conductor blink or Eagles resets Innocence.
#6Enduring Curiosity / Enduring Innocence (return trigger)
"When Enduring Curiosity dies, if it was a creature, return it to the battlefield under its owner's control. It's an enchantment." (Innocence has the identical clause.)
WhyIf we kill an Enduring card without blanking it first, it returns as an enchantment that STILL has its draw trigger (Curiosity keeps drawing off combat damage). Countering the return leaves the card in the graveyard permanently with no dependence on Tidebinder surviving.
WhenAfter it dies (our Bitter Triumph / Shoot the Sheriff / combat), with the return trigger on the stack. Source is a card in the graveyard, so nothing loses abilities (ruling 3), but the counter is permanent. Under High Noon the removal spell plus Tidebinder is two spells in one turn, so this needs the kill and the counter on different players' turns or no High Noon; under Voice/Walters both must happen on our turn.
AfterThe Enduring card stays in the graveyard as a dead card. Tidebinder is a 3/2 body.
RiskCosts 3 mana on top of the removal spell; Ghost Vacuum cannot save it (exiling it from the graveyard just changes where the dead card sits).
#7Voice of Victory (Mobilize 2 attack trigger, lock OFF only)
"Mobilize 2 (Whenever this creature attacks, create two tapped and attacking 1/1 red Warrior creature tokens. Sacrifice them at the beginning of the next end step.)"
WhyOnce one lock piece is blanked or dead and a SECOND Voice attacks, its Mobilize trigger is a triggered ability of a creature on the battlefield: countering it blanks that Voice too, deleting its "Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn" for as long as Tidebinder lives, and the two Warriors are never created. Voice is a 2-of, so double-lock boards happen.
WhenTheir declare attackers step, only when no OTHER live lock piece is on the battlefield (a lone unblanked Voice locks us out of its own trigger).
AfterThat Voice is a 1/3 with no abilities while Tidebinder lives: no lock, no Mobilize. No tokens.
RiskRequires the lock to already be off; Erode/Aang/Conductor on Tidebinder re-locks their turn.
#8Enduring Curiosity (combat draw)
"Whenever a creature you control deals combat damage to a player, draw a card."
WhyCountering one draw trigger blanks the whole card: no more draws and no dies-return, so a follow-up Bitter Triumph or Shoot the Sheriff (Cat Glimmer, not an outlaw) leaves it dead. Ranked low because the trigger happens only on their turn after an unblocked hit, is impossible under Voice/Walters, and is our whole spell for their turn under High Noon. Wasp blank-then-kill is the usual way to handle Curiosity.
WhenTheir combat damage step, draw trigger on the stack, no lock piece live. One Tidebinder counters one trigger; other unblocked attackers' draws still resolve.
AfterCuriosity stays a 4/3 with no abilities. Cards already drawn stay. Kill it while blanked and it stays dead.
RiskConductor blink, Eagles, Erode on Tidebinder all reset it.
#9The Wondrous Wasp (theirs, 1 main)
"Wasp's Sting: When The Wondrous Wasp enters, tap up to one target creature. It loses all abilities for as long as The Wondrous Wasp remains on the battlefield."
WhyTheir Wasp on our Enduring Curiosity or Wan Shi Tong shuts our engine off. Counter it and their Wasp is a 2/1 ground body that blanks nothing.
WhenWhenever they flash it in (usually our turn or our end step). Worth it only when the target is Curiosity/Wan Shi Tong or the Wasp/Tidebinder holding their lock piece down. Their Wasp aimed at our Tidebinder or Wasp is mostly a tap: our effects last as long as our creature remains on the battlefield, not as long as it has abilities.
AfterTheir Wasp stays a vanilla 2/1 while Tidebinder lives; nothing of ours is tapped or blanked.
RiskLow; 1-of.
#10Ghost Vacuum
"{T}: Exile target card from a graveyard." and "{6}, {T}, Sacrifice this artifact: Put each creature card exiled with this artifact onto the battlefield under your control..."
WhyThe free tap ability (no mana needed) is how they exile our Enduring Curiosity from the graveyard in response to its return trigger. Countering that saves the return and blanks Vacuum (an artifact) while Tidebinder lives, so it can never be used again. The 6-mana ability with our Beavers/Curiosity/Wan Shi Tong underneath is a blowout to counter: Vacuum is already sacrificed and the cards stay exiled.
WhenExile activation: in response, typically the moment our Curiosity dies; they need no open mana for it. 6-mana ability: sorcery speed on their turn, so only reachable with no lock piece live.
AfterExile mode countered: Vacuum stays tapped on the battlefield with no abilities until Tidebinder leaves. Sac mode countered: Vacuum is gone, exiled cards stay exiled.
Risk1-of main, 1 more in side; long games only.
#11Restless Anchorage
"{1}{W}{U}: Until end of turn, this land becomes a 2/3 white and blue Bird creature with flying." and "Whenever this land attacks, create a Map token."
WhyOpportunistic 1-of. Countering the animate ability wastes their 3 mana but the land is not a creature as the trigger resolves, so it keeps its abilities (ruling 3). Countering the ATTACK trigger is better: it is a creature at that moment, so the permanent loses ALL abilities while Tidebinder lives, including its mana ability, even after it turns back into a land.
WhenDeclare attackers on their turn, so only with no lock piece live.
AfterAttack trigger countered: no Map token, the 2/3 loses flying this turn, and afterward Anchorage cannot tap for mana or animate while Tidebinder lives. Animate countered: land untouched, 3 mana wasted.
RiskRarely worth holding for.
#12Abandoned Air Temple
"{3}{W}, {T}: Put a +1/+1 counter on each creature you control."
WhyFour mana plus tapping the Temple itself versus three of ours, and it stops a lethal alpha. The land is not a creature so nothing is blanked.
WhenUsually their precombat main or combat, so only without a lock piece. 2 copies.
AfterNo counters placed; Air Temple keeps its mana ability. Tidebinder is a 3/2 body.
RiskRare activation in a tempo deck.
#13Seam Rip (side)
"When this enchantment enters, exile target nonland permanent an opponent controls with mana value 2 or less until this enchantment leaves the battlefield."
WhyOnly worth countering when the target is the Wasp holding Voice/Walters blanked (losing it re-locks their turn) or a Floodpits Drowner mid-line. Enchantment source: nothing loses abilities, the exile simply does not happen.
WhenTheir main phase (sorcery-speed enchantment), so we need no live lock piece on their side, which is exactly the state when Wasp is holding one down. Post-board only.
AfterOur permanent stays; Seam Rip sits on the battlefield doing nothing.
RiskThree mana to protect a 2-drop is poor unless that drop is the lock-breaker.
Traps
Voice of Victory / Jennifer Walters: Tidebinder counters "Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn." That is a static ability; only activated and triggered abilities can be targeted. While a lock piece is on the battlefield we cannot cast Tidebinder on their turn at all. Tidebinder cannot touch the lock; Requiting Hex ({B}, MV 2 or less), Shoot the Sheriff (Human Bard / Human Advisor Hero are not outlaws), Bitter Triumph, our own Drowner stun-then-shuffle, or Wasp's blank can. The only Tidebinder line is countering a SECOND Voice's Mobilize trigger after the first lock piece is already off.
High Noon: Counter High Noon or its ability. "Each player can't cast more than one spell each turn" is static. Its {4}{R} ability needs red mana they lack; and since sacrificing High Noon is part of the cost, countering it would only stop the 5 damage, the enchantment is already gone. Practical effect on us: once we cast a spell on our turn, Tidebinder cannot be the response to their flash play that turn. The limit is per player per turn, so our turn's spell and their turn's spell are separate budgets.
Aven Interrupter: Counter the {2} tax, or expect the countered ETB to remove Interrupter. "Spells your opponents cast from graveyards or from exile cost {2} more to cast" is static and cannot be targeted; Tidebinder removes it only as a side effect of blanking the creature after countering its ETB. Countering an ETB never removes the permanent: Interrupter stays as a body.
Skycoach Conductor: Tidebinder stops the blink, or stops it entering prepared, or blanking Conductor turns the blink off. "This creature enters prepared" is not a triggered ability. Casting the prepared copy of All Aboard ("Exile target non-Pilot creature you control, then return that card to the battlefield") is casting a SPELL. And Scryfall ruling 2026-03-20: "If a prepared creature loses all abilities, it won't stop being prepared, and nothing will happen to ... the copy of its prepare spell in exile." Only a counterspell on the copy (Spell Pierce, We Say Thee Nay!; not Spell Snare, the copy's MV is 1) or removing Conductor before they cast it stops the blink. The returned creature is a new object: every blank and stun counter on it is gone.
Bilbo's Gambit: Counter the Gift Treasure or the bounce. Gambit is an instant; "Return target spell to its owner's hand" and the Gift Treasure creation are spell effects, not abilities. If the gift was promised, "players can't cast spells this turn" then shuts off our flash for the rest of that turn.
Settle the Wreckage / Erode / Avatar's Wrath / Pyrrhic Strike / Spell Pierce / Disdainful Stroke / Flashfreeze: Counter the basic-land search, the airbend, or the exile. All spell effects. Erode's and Settle's land searches, Avatar's Wrath's mass airbend and its "opponents can't cast spells from anywhere other than their hands" clause are parts of spells resolving. Tidebinder never interacts with a spell.
Clarion Conqueror (side): Counter "Activated abilities of artifacts, creatures, and planeswalkers can't be activated." Static. Tidebinder cannot target it. Wasp blanks it (see Wasp list) or Bitter Triumph / Shoot the Sheriff (Dragon is not an outlaw) kill it outright; Day of Black Sun with X of 3 or more also gets it.
Hallowed Fountain / Multiversal Passage / Abandoned Air Temple / Temple of Enlightenment (enters tapped): Counter the shock-land life payment or the basic-type choice. "As this land enters, you may pay 2 life" and "As this land enters, choose a basic land type" are replacement effects; "This land enters tapped" is not a trigger. Temple's "When this land enters, scry 1" IS a trigger but is not worth 3 mana.
Restless Anchorage (animate): Countering the animate ability blanks the land. Tidebinder checks the source's type as its trigger resolves (ruling 3). The land has not become a creature yet, so it keeps all abilities; they only lose the 3 mana. The attack trigger, by contrast, does blank it.
Any mana ability (lands, Gambit's Treasure): Target a mana ability. "(Mana abilities can't be targeted.)" is printed on Tidebinder.
Not worth it
Temple of Enlightenment scry 1: 3 mana for a scry is never right.Aang's "Waterbend {8}: Transform Aang": counterable (tapped permanents stay tapped), but they almost never activate it; do not hold Tidebinder for it.Aang and La's attack trigger: only exists after an 8-mana transform.Jennifer Walters' She-Hulk back face: she cannot transform without green mana.Mobilize Warrior tokens' delayed "Sacrifice them at the beginning of the next end step" trigger: counterable, but it only helps THEM (the tokens survive). Never target it.Petrified Hamlet ETB ("choose a land card name"): countering it means no name is chosen, so its lockout and granted-{C} clauses do nothing, but Hamlet still taps for {C} itself; a 1-of side land aimed at our Restless Reef at best.Restless Anchorage's Map token by itself: only take it when the blank-the-land bonus matters or nothing else is coming.Ghost Vacuum's returned 1/1 Spirits (if the {6} mode ever resolves): they carry their original text again, but this line almost never happens; handle them like any small creature.
The Wondrous Wasp
Wasp is a strong 2-mana play here but not our best card in the matchup; Requiting Hex ({B}, 4 main, "Destroy target creature with mana value 2 or less") kills Voice of Victory or Jennifer Walters permanently for one mana and Kaito dodges their entire ETB suite. Every creature in their 75 is a legal Wasp target (no hexproof, no ward), and the blank has two jobs Hex cannot do: (1) turn a lock piece off on OUR turn when no removal is in hand, reopening their turn to Tidebinder, We Say Thee Nay!, Spell Snare and Bitter Triumph; (2) blank an Enduring card BEFORE killing it, because Curiosity or Innocence killed unblanked returns as an enchantment that still draws cards. Post-board it also switches off Clarion Conqueror so Kaito can activate again. The blank is fragile: Aang airbending Wasp (3), Skycoach Conductor blinking the blanked creature (4), The Eagles Are Coming! bouncing it (2), Erode (1 main + 2 side), Avatar's Wrath (2), Floodpits Drowner stun-then-shuffle (2), Seam Rip (side, Wasp is MV 2), and Settle the Wreckage if Wasp ever attacks. Pyrrhic Strike cannot hit Wasp (MV 2). Expect any blank to last about one turn cycle; kill the blanked creature while the window is open.
#1Voice of Victory
WhyBlank value, not tap value. "Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn" closes their whole turn to us and shields every other target in this guide; blanking Voice also deletes Mobilize 2 ("Whenever this creature attacks, create two tapped and attacking 1/1 red Warrior creature tokens"). Two copies. Use Wasp when Requiting Hex / Bitter Triumph / Shoot the Sheriff are not in hand or are needed elsewhere; removal is permanent, the blank is not.
WhenOUR turn (main phase or our end step; any time we have priority on our own turn), because while Voice is out we cannot cast Wasp on theirs. The tap is irrelevant (it untaps in their untap step); the blank is everything. On the draw Voice often lands before our turn 2, so turn-2 Wasp on Voice (or turn-2 Hex) is the play.
AfterVoice stays a 1/3 with no abilities while our Wasp remains on the battlefield: no lock, no Mobilize tokens. Their turn is open to our instants and flash. Ends immediately if Wasp is killed, exiled, bounced, airbended, or if we ninjutsu it away.
#2Jennifer Walters
WhyIdentical lock text: "Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn." Her "{3}{G}{W}{W}: Transform" is uncastable in a WU deck, so the lock is her whole card; blanked, she is a vanilla 2/3 (blocks and kills our Wasp and survives Spyglass Siren). 1 copy. Same Hex-first logic as Voice.
WhenOur turn, same as Voice. If both Voice and Walters are out, one Wasp is not enough; pair with Requiting Hex/Bitter Triumph/Shoot the Sheriff on the other, or wait for the second Wasp.
AfterWalters stays a 2/3 with no abilities while Wasp lives; their turn is open again.
#3Enduring Curiosity (theirs)
WhyBlank value and tap value both. Blanked, it stops drawing ("Whenever a creature you control deals combat damage to a player, draw a card") and loses its dies-return clause, so our next Bitter Triumph / Shoot the Sheriff kills it permanently. Killed WITHOUT the blank it comes back as an enchantment that still draws. As their biggest body (4/3), it is also the best tap-before-attackers target when no lock piece is out.
WhenBlank: any time on our turn; their end step only if no lock piece is live. Tap-before-attackers: their beginning of combat, only with no Voice/Walters on the battlefield; a tap after attackers are declared does not remove it from combat. Then kill it while blanked, ideally the same turn, before Conductor/Eagles/Aang resets it.
After4/3 with no abilities: no draws, and if it dies while Wasp is on the battlefield it stays dead. Cards it already drew stay drawn. If Wasp leaves before it dies, the return clause is back.
#4Enduring Innocence
WhyTheir other engine ("Whenever one or more other creatures you control with power 2 or less enter, draw a card") in a deck where every main-deck creature except Curiosity has power 2 or less, plus lifelink. Blank then kill = permanent; killed unblanked it returns as an enchantment that still draws.
WhenOur turn, ideally before their next flash creature enters (the draw trigger fires when they flash in on our turn). Kill it while blanked.
After2/1 with no abilities: no lifelink, no draws, no return while Wasp lives.
#5Clarion Conqueror (side)
Why"Activated abilities of artifacts, creatures, and planeswalkers can't be activated" shuts off Kaito's loyalty abilities (creature on our turn, planeswalker on theirs, covered either way) and our Floodpits Drowner's shuffle. Tidebinder cannot touch a static; Wasp blanks it, or Bitter Triumph / Shoot the Sheriff kills it. It is also a 3/3 flyer that eats our Wasp in combat, so tapping it on our turn lets Wasp/Siren attack.
WhenOur turn, before we want to activate Kaito. Post-board only. Kaito's ninjutsu should be unaffected either way: Conqueror names artifacts/creatures/planeswalkers, which means permanents on the battlefield, and Kaito in hand is a card, not a permanent (rule-derived, not a card ruling in our data).
After3/3 with no flying and no lock on activated abilities while Wasp lives. Kaito's +1/0/-2 and our Drowner's shuffle work again.
#6Bird Soldier token (from The Eagles Are Coming!)
WhyA 4/4 flyer is their biggest attacker. Tapping it at their beginning of combat (lock off) stops an attack; blanking it removes flying so Curiosity/Beavers/Drowner can block it and it stops being a flying blocker for our Siren/Wasp.
WhenTheir beginning of combat when no lock piece is live, or our turn to remove it as a flying blocker / neuter it before their attack.
After4/4 ground vanilla while Wasp lives. Killing a token is permanent anyway; Bitter Triumph on it is often better if we have it.
#7Aven Interrupter
WhyIts ETB already happened, so the blank removes flying and the static "Spells your opponents cast from graveyards or from exile cost {2} more to cast". If they plotted our Kaito/Curiosity, blanking Interrupter lets us cast the plotted card for free (plotted is a designation, unaffected by the blank). Also clears a flying blocker.
WhenOur main phase, the turn we cast the plotted card (a plotted card is cast as a sorcery on a later turn). Check no second Interrupter is out; each taxes separately.
After2/2 with no abilities: no flying, no tax. Our plotted card still needs Avatar's Wrath's "opponents can't cast spells from anywhere other than their hands" not to be in effect.
#8Aang, Swift Savior
WhyAirbend already resolved by the time we can act; blanking removes flying and Waterbend {8}, making a 2/3 ground body our Curiosity walls. Best as a tap-before-attackers target when no lock is out, or a blocker-tap on our turn.
WhenTheir beginning of combat (no lock piece) to keep him home; or our beginning of combat to remove a flying blocker so Spyglass Siren connects for a ninjutsu.
After2/3 with no flash/flying/airbend/waterbend while Wasp lives. Anything already airbended stays exiled (we still recast for {2}).
#9Floodpits Drowner (theirs)
WhyIf they have stunned one of our creatures and hold {1}{U}, blanking Drowner removes "{1}{U}, {T}: Shuffle this creature and target creature with a stun counter on it into their owners' libraries" before they fire it. Cheaper than holding Tidebinder for the activation, and Requiting Hex kills it outright too.
WhenOur turn, before they untap with the mana to activate. Only when they actually have a stunned target of ours (Kaito can never be one).
After2/1 with no abilities: no vigilance, no shuffle. The stun counter on our creature stays and still expires normally.
#10Skycoach Conductor
WhyTap value only: a 2/3 flying vigilance blocker that walls Wasp and Spyglass Siren. Tapping it at our beginning of combat lets a flyer through for a Kaito ninjutsu. The blank does NOT stop the prepared blink (Scryfall ruling 2026-03-20).
WhenOur beginning of combat, the turn we want an unblocked flyer.
AfterTapped this turn; loses flying and vigilance while Wasp lives. Its All Aboard copy is still castable.
#11Restless Anchorage (while animated)
WhyNiche 1-of. If they animate it (2/3 flying Bird until end of turn) to attack on their turn with the lock off, or to block on our turn, it is a creature and Wasp can target it: the permanent loses all abilities for as long as Wasp remains, including its mana ability and animate ability even after it reverts to a land. Also removes flying so it cannot block our Siren.
WhenOnly while it is a creature: their beginning of combat after they animate (lock off), or on our turn if they animate to block.
AfterReverts to a land at end of turn but stays a dead land (no mana, no animate, no Map trigger) while Wasp lives.
Traps
Enduring Curiosity / Enduring Innocence: Just kill it with Bitter Triumph or Shoot the Sheriff; no need to blank first. "When ... dies, if it was a creature, return it to the battlefield ... It's an enchantment." The returned enchantment keeps its draw trigger (Curiosity keeps drawing off combat damage; Innocence keeps drawing off small creatures entering). Blank with Wasp first (or counter the return trigger with Tidebinder), then kill.
The Wondrous Wasp (theirs): Blank their Wasp to free the creature of ours it blanked. Their effect lasts "for as long as The Wondrous Wasp remains on the battlefield", not for as long as it has abilities. Kill it (Requiting Hex, Bitter Triumph, Shoot the Sheriff), bounce it, or stun-and-shuffle it with our own Drowner. The same holds in reverse: their Wasp on our Tidebinder or Wasp does not turn off our blank.
Voice of Victory / Jennifer Walters: Flash Wasp on their turn to tap Voice before attacks; or Wasp on Voice lets us cast two spells this turn. While the lock piece is on the battlefield we cannot cast any spell on their turn, so Wasp must be cast on OUR turn, where the tap is irrelevant and the blank is the whole play. And the lock only ever affected THEIR turn: unlocking Voice opens their turn to our instants, it never adds a spell to our own turn (that limit is High Noon's, which Wasp cannot touch).
Any attacker: Wasp after attackers are declared to stop the attack. Tapping an attacking creature does not remove it from combat. To stop an attack Wasp must resolve in their beginning of combat step (or earlier), which is only possible when no lock piece is live.
Skycoach Conductor: Blank Conductor to stop the blink. Scryfall ruling 2026-03-20: "If a prepared creature loses all abilities, it won't stop being prepared, and nothing will happen to ... the copy of its prepare spell in exile." The blink is live regardless; counter the All Aboard copy (Spell Pierce, We Say Thee Nay!) or kill Conductor first.
High Noon: Wasp answers the one-spell lock. High Noon is an enchantment, not a creature; Wasp only targets creatures. Under High Noon, Wasp + Tidebinder in the same turn is impossible. Kaito's ninjutsu is an activated ability, not a spell, so it does not count toward the one spell.
Aang / Aven Interrupter: Blanking undoes the airbend or the plot. Effects of an ability that already resolved stay. Our airbended card stays exiled (recast for {2}, +{2} per unblanked Interrupter); our plotted card stays plotted (castable free as a sorcery later; blanking Interrupter only removes the {2} tax).
Kaito, Bane of Nightmares (ours): Use Wasp as the ninjutsu enabler while it holds Voice down, or alpha with Wasp into 4 open mana. Ninjutsu returns the unblocked attacker to hand; Wasp leaving ends the blank and Voice's lock returns immediately. Settle the Wreckage ("Exile all attacking creatures target player controls") targets the player, so it exiles an attacking Wasp (and an attacking Kaito through hexproof) and re-locks their turn. Enable ninjutsu with Spyglass Siren and leave Wasp home.
Lines with the rest of the deck
  • Break the lock, then punish. Order of preference on Voice of Victory / Jennifer Walters: Requiting Hex ({B}, permanent) > Bitter Triumph / Shoot the Sheriff (permanent, 2 mana) > our Floodpits Drowner (stun on our turn, then "{1}{U}, {T}: Shuffle", an ability, so it works on their turn even under the lock) > Wasp blank (2 mana, fragile). Once their turn is open: Tidebinder on a Drowner activation / Curiosity draw / second Voice's Mobilize, We Say Thee Nay! or Spell Snare (MV 2: Voice, Walters, High Noon, Drowner, Gambit, Eagles unkicked) on their spells, Bitter Triumph at instant speed. Keep the blanking Wasp out of combat.
  • Blank-then-kill the Enduring cards: Wasp (or a Tidebinder counter of the draw trigger) on Enduring Curiosity/Innocence, then Bitter Triumph or Shoot the Sheriff the same turn while blanked. Killed unblanked, it returns as an enchantment that still draws. If we killed it first, Tidebinder can counter the return trigger (no blank, but the counter is permanent).
  • Protect the resolved Wan Shi Tong: when Aang flashes in at our end step targeting Wan Shi Tong or Curiosity, Tidebinder counters the airbend trigger (our turn, so Voice does not stop us; High Noon does if we already cast a spell). An airbended Wan Shi Tong recasts with X=0 as a 1/1 that draws nothing, so this is the single most valuable Tidebinder moment in the matchup.
  • Bait the Interrupter (late game only): with 3 spare mana and no High Noon, cast the spell we are willing to lose; when the Aven Interrupter or Aang ETB targets it, flash Tidebinder on that trigger. Watch for a second flash answer: while Tidebinder's trigger is on the stack our spell is still there for another Interrupter/Aang to take.
  • Eagles timing (only with no Voice/Walters on the battlefield): cast Bitter Triumph at THEIR end step. If they respond with The Eagles Are Coming!, the 4/4 token trigger lands in OUR upkeep and one Tidebinder stops every token (under High Noon that is our whole turn's spell; sometimes eating a 4/4 beats skipping Kaito). If a lock forces us to remove on our own turn, the tokens land in their upkeep and are uncounterable for us.
  • Stop the reset: their cleanest undo of any blank or stun is Skycoach Conductor's All Aboard copy ({U}, MV 1 instant). Spell Pierce (side) or We Say Thee Nay! counters it; Spell Snare cannot. The returned creature is a new object with no counters, so shuffle a stunned creature with our Drowner at the first opportunity rather than waiting.
  • High Noon budgets: the limit is one spell per player per turn. Spend OUR turn's spell on development (Kaito via ninjutsu costs none; Curiosity, Beavers, Wan Shi Tong) and THEIR turn's spell (lock off) on Tidebinder at end step, Bitter Triumph, or a counterspell. Never 'cast nothing main phase' to hold Tidebinder.
  • Kaito: on our turn he is a hexproof creature, on theirs a planeswalker, so Aang's airbend, Drowner's stun, their Wasp, Pyrrhic Strike and Avatar's Wrath never touch him on the battlefield. The Kaito SPELL is exposed to Interrupter, Aang, Gambit, Spell Pierce and Disdainful Stroke, so ninjutsu him in off an unblocked Spyglass Siren (Wasp tapping their flying blocker at our beginning of combat helps) and no spell is cast; ninjutsu also ignores High Noon. What does kill him: Erode on their turn ("Destroy target creature or planeswalker", 1 main + 2 side) and Settle the Wreckage while he attacks (targets the player, ignores hexproof). Never alpha into 4 open white mana.
  • Answer their Wasp the right way: their Wondrous Wasp blanking our Curiosity/Wan Shi Tong is fixed by Requiting Hex, Bitter Triumph, Shoot the Sheriff, or our Drowner (stun, then shuffle), never by our Wasp or Tidebinder blanking it.
  • Post-board: Wasp on Clarion Conqueror (or Bitter Triumph / Shoot the Sheriff on it) reopens Kaito's loyalty abilities and our Drowner's shuffle. Pyrrhic Strike kills Tidebinder (MV 3) but not Wasp (MV 2): prefer Wasp for blanks that need to last, hold Tidebinder as the responder. Tidebinder on Seam Rip's ETB only when Seam Rip is aimed at the Wasp holding Voice/Walters down.
Hold or fire
WASP: when Voice of Victory or Jennifer Walters is out and we have no Requiting Hex / Bitter Triumph / Shoot the Sheriff (or the removal is needed for a Drowner or Curiosity), fire Wasp at the lock piece on our turn; removal is preferred because the blank dies to Aang, Conductor, Eagles and Erode within a turn cycle. With no lock piece out, hold Wasp for Enduring Curiosity/Innocence and kill the blanked creature the same turn; tap-before-attackers on Curiosity or a 4/4 Bird only when their turn is open; a bare 2/1 flyer body is the last resort. Never attack with, or ninjutsu away, a Wasp that is holding a lock piece. TIDEBINDER: do not hold it all game for the 7-mana Interrupter line. Fire it (a) at Aang's airbend aimed at a resolved Wan Shi Tong/Curiosity or at the Wasp holding a blank, (b) at a Floodpits Drowner ETB aimed at a creature we care about (blanks the shuffle too), (c) at an Eagles trigger in our upkeep, (d) at an Innocence draw or the Enduring return trigger, (e) at a second Voice's Mobilize once the first lock is off; otherwise cast it as a 3/2 at THEIR end step whenever no lock piece is live. Under High Noon: develop on our turn, use their turn's spell for Tidebinder or removal; do not skip our main phase. ON THE PLAY: develop normally turns 2-4 (Siren, Beavers, Drowner, Kaito via ninjutsu); our turn-2 Wasp lands before their Voice, so keep Wasp for Voice/Walters or an Enduring card and keep Hex for the lock piece; Tidebinder goes in at their end step or in response to a Drowner/Aang ETB, not on a 7-mana plan. ON THE DRAW: expect Voice/Walters or High Noon before our turn 2; Hex or Wasp on the lock is the turn-2 play, and expect Aang/Erode/Conductor to undo a Wasp blank, so keep the second answer for the re-locked creature rather than committing Kaito's spell into a closed turn.
How many to run
Main deck: keep stock (2 The Wondrous Wasp, 1 Tishana's Tidebinder main, 2 Tidebinder side). Wasp is good here (every creature is a legal target, two lock pieces, four Enduring engines that must be blanked before dying, Conqueror post-board) and 2 is the right count; Requiting Hex already covers the lock pieces permanently, so a third Wasp adds little. Tidebinder is situational: only 10 of their 21 creatures carry an ETB trigger, 4 High Noon and 3 lock creatures gate when it can be cast, and its 3/2 ground body cannot block their flyers and trades poorly into 2/3 vigilance Conductor, 1/3 Voice and 2/3 Walters. POST-BOARD vs this deck: bring in ONE side Tidebinder (2 total), not both. The second slot is better spent on Spell Pierce x2 (counters High Noon, Eagles, Gambit, Erode, Settle, Avatar's Wrath and the All Aboard blink copy that resets our blanks) and Duress (High Noon, Settle, Wrath, Eagles, Gambit); Annul is a live candidate over the third Tidebinder since it counters High Noon, Seam Rip, Ghost Vacuum AND the Enduring Curiosity/Innocence spells (enchantment creature spells), including a Curiosity flashed in at our end step. Do not cut Spell Snare (MV 2: Voice, Walters, High Noon, Drowner, Gambit, unkicked Eagles) or all of Requiting Hex (it kills Voice, Walters, Drowner and their Wasp). Which specific cards leave depends on the exact list and is outside the Tidebinder/Wasp question.
Review trail: confidence medium, 9 disputed, 17 changes
Disputed reviewer points (kept as written)
  • Reviewer 2 (missed targets): 'Kaito's loyalty/ninjutsu and Restless Reef all still work on their turn' under the lock. Partly wrong: loyalty abilities are activated only as a sorcery (CR 606.3), so they never worked on their turn, and ninjutsu is a combat action on our turn. The correct claim, which the page now makes, is that Voice/Walters stop SPELLS only: our Drowner's shuffle and Restless Reef's animation (activated abilities) still work on their turn.
  • Reviewer 2 (Wasp verdict): 'lead with Wasp on Voice only when it lets us cast a second spell the same turn.' Wrong framing: Voice's text is 'Your opponents can't cast spells during YOUR turn' (theirs). Blanking Voice never adds a spell to our own turn; the one-spell-per-turn limit is High Noon's, which Wasp cannot target. Wasp on Voice is judged by whether it reopens THEIR turn.
  • Reviewer 3: 'Aang's P/T is null in the file and cannot be assumed' and 'Walters' P/T is unverified.' Resolved by direct Scryfall fetch on 2026-09-11 (api.scryfall.com/cards/named): Aang, Swift Savior 2/3 (Aang and La 5/5), Jennifer Walters 2/3 (She-Hulk 6/6), Skycoach Conductor 2/3. The page states 2/3 for both and drops the 'not in data file' hedges.
  • Earlier-round claim rejected: 'Tidebinder can counter Voice of Victory's / Jennifer Walters' lock.' Static ability; Tidebinder targets only activated or triggered abilities (card text), and while the lock is live we cannot cast Tidebinder on their turn at all.
  • Earlier-round claim rejected: 'Countering Aven Interrupter's or Aang's ETB removes the creature.' Countering an ETB removes only the ability from the stack; the permanent stays and, being a creature, loses all abilities while Tidebinder remains (card text + rules brief).
  • Earlier-round claim rejected: 'Blanking their Wondrous Wasp frees our creature.' Their effect lasts 'for as long as The Wondrous Wasp remains on the battlefield' (oracle text), a zone condition, not an ability condition.
  • Earlier-round claim rejected: 'Countering Restless Anchorage's animate ability blanks the land.' Tidebinder ruling 3: the type check happens as the trigger resolves; the land is not yet a creature, so it keeps its abilities.
  • Earlier-round claim rejected: 'Wasp flashed in after attackers are declared stops the attack.' Tapping an attacking creature does not remove it from combat (rules brief); and under a live lock piece Wasp cannot be cast on their turn anyway.
  • Earlier-round claim rejected: 'Kaito can be stunned by their Drowner or airbended by Aang on the battlefield.' Scryfall ruling 2024-09-20: Kaito stops being a planeswalker on our turn (and has hexproof); on their turn he is a planeswalker, not a creature, so 'target creature' effects cannot select him. Only the Kaito spell is exposed, and Erode/Settle the Wreckage are the cards that do kill him.
Changes after review
  • Re-read /Users/emersonspartz/dimir-tidebinder-wasp-guide/data/decks/standard-azorius-tempo-woe.json (all 31 cards) and pulled our own cards' oracle text (Requiting Hex, Shoot the Sheriff, Bitter Triumph, Kaito, Wan Shi Tong, Spell Snare, Annul, Duress, Day of Black Sun, Faebloom Trick, We Say Thee Nay!) from /Users/emersonspartz/dimir-tidebinder-wasp-guide/data/opponents.json.
  • Verified on Scryfall (2026-09-11): Aang 2/3, Jennifer Walters 2/3, Skycoach Conductor 2/3; Conductor ruling 2026-03-20 that a prepared creature losing all abilities stays prepared and the blinked creature returns as a new object with counters gone; Aang airbend ruling that an X spell recast from exile must choose X=0; Kaito ruling that he stops being a planeswalker on our turn.

Removed 'only Wasp turns the lock off' and 'nothing else in our 75 removes it at 2 mana' everywhere; rewrote hold_or_fire so removal is preferred and Wasp is the backup unlock.
  • MAJOR fix: Aven Interrupter risk line corrected: a second Interrupter/Aang flashed in while Tidebinder's trigger is on the stack can target our original spell (still on the stack) or the Tidebinder spell itself.
  • MAJOR fix: added Tidebinder target 'Voice of Victory Mobilize 2 attack trigger' (lock off only): countering it blanks a second Voice and removes its lock.
  • MAJOR fix: counts corrected to 21 creatures main, 16 flash, 10 with a counterable ETB; Skycoach Conductor has no triggered/activated ability. Verdict downgraded: Tidebinder from 'good' to 'situational', Wasp from 'premium' to 'good', with reasoning.

High Noon advice changed from 'cast nothing main phase' to per-player-per-turn budgets. Incoherent 'turn 4 / 7 mana' on-the-play text deleted and rewritten.

  • Clarion Conqueror: Tidebinder trap and Wasp target now say Bitter Triumph / Shoot the Sheriff / Day of Black Sun X>=3 also answer it; the ninjutsu-unaffected claim is now labelled rule-derived (creature = permanent on the battlefield), not card-ruling-verified.
